    assert manager is not None
    assert manager.contains("area.central")
    assert manager.has_central()


# ----- clear and reload ---------------------------------------------


def test_clear_drops_composition(workspace: Workspace) -> None:
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    workspace.load_spec(spec)
    assert workspace.is_loaded() is True

    workspace.clear()

    assert workspace.is_loaded() is False
    assert workspace.areas() == ()
    assert workspace.area_manager() is None


def test_clear_on_unloaded_workspace_is_noop(
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    workspace.clear()  # must not raise
    assert workspace.is_loaded() is False


def test_clear_preserves_top_overlay(workspace: Workspace) -> None:
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    workspace.load_spec(spec)

    overlay_before = workspace.top_overlay()

    workspace.clear()

    # The TopOverlay instance is stable; the OverlayManager still
    # holds it.
    assert workspace.top_overlay() is overlay_before
    assert workspace.overlay_manager().contains("overlay.top_bar")


def test_reload_replaces_composition(workspace: Workspace) -> None:
    spec_a = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    spec_b = _spec(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.object_list"),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.viewport_3d"),
        _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.properties"),
    )

    workspace.load_spec(spec_a)
    workspace.load_spec(spec_b)

    ids = sorted(a.area_id for a in workspace.areas())
    assert ids == ["area.central", "area.left", "area.right"]


def test_reload_keeps_top_overlay_registered(
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))

    workspace.load_spec(spec)
    workspace.load_spec(spec)

    assert workspace.overlay_manager().contains("overlay.top_bar")


# ----- Overlays and regions -----------------------------------------


def test_load_spec_triggers_overlay_repositioning(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    """After a load + resize, the TopOverlay has non-zero geometry.

    The TopOverlay is anchored to the Central Region. If the Central
    Area has a non-zero size, the TopOverlay must have a non-zero
    width.
    """
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    workspace.resize(800, 600)
    workspace.show()
    qtbot.waitExposed(workspace)

    workspace.load_spec(spec)
    workspace.resize(800, 600)  # trigger a resizeEvent

    overlay_widget = workspace.top_overlay().widget()
    # The overlay must have received some bounds; width may vary with
    # Qt's layout timing, but the widget must be visible.
    assert overlay_widget.isVisible() is True


def test_workspace_resize_does_not_crash_unloaded(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    workspace.show()
    qtbot.waitExposed(workspace)
    workspace.resize(500, 400)  # no load; must not raise


def test_top_overlay_is_above_root_widget(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    """The TopOverlay floats above the rendered composition.

    The TopOverlay widget is a child of the Workspace and is raised
    above the composition's root widget. It must not be reparented
    into the root widget.
    """
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    workspace.resize(800, 600)
    workspace.show()
    qtbot.waitExposed(workspace)

    workspace.load_spec(spec)

    # The TopOverlay's widget's parent is the Workspace itself.
    overlay_widget = workspace.top_overlay().widget()
    assert overlay_widget.parent() is workspace


# ----- Overlay manager stability ------------------------------------


def test_overlay_manager_is_stable_across_load_spec(
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    manager_before = workspace.overlay_manager()
    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    workspace.load_spec(spec)
    manager_after = workspace.overlay_manager()
    assert manager_before is manager_after


def test_overlay_manager_is_stable_across_clear(
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    manager_before = workspace.overlay_manager()
    workspace.clear()
    manager_after = workspace.overlay_manager()
    assert manager_before is manager_after


def test_top_overlay_is_positioned_over_central_area(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    workspace: Workspace,
) -> None:
    """The TopOverlay must be positioned over the Central Area.

    This is a regression test for the bug where the TopOverlay was
    positioned at (0, 0) with zero width when the splitter was moved,
    instead of following the Central Area's geometry.
    """
    spec = _spec(
        _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.object_list"),
        _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.viewport_3d"),
        _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.properties"),
    )
    workspace.resize(1200, 800)
    workspace.load_spec(spec)
    workspace.show()
    qtbot.waitExposed(workspace)

    # Find the Central Area widget first, so the waitUntil condition
    # can compare against its actual position.
    central_widget = None
    for area in workspace.areas():
        if area.area_id == "area.central":
            for widget in workspace.findChildren(QWidget):
                if hasattr(widget, "area_id") and widget.area_id == "area.central":
                    central_widget = widget
                    break
            break

    assert central_widget is not None, "Central Area widget must exist"

    # Wait until the TopOverlay is ACTUALLY positioned inside the
    # Central Area. Checking x > 0 is insufficient because the
    # TopOverlay may be positioned at x=8 (with bounds from the
    # old layout) before the Qt layout is complete.
    def _overlay_is_over_central() -> bool:
        overlay_geom = workspace.top_overlay().widget().geometry()
        central_top_left = central_widget.mapTo(workspace, central_widget.rect().topLeft())
        central_right = central_top_left.x() + central_widget.width()
        return (
            overlay_geom.x() >= central_top_left.x()
            and overlay_geom.x() + overlay_geom.width() <= central_right
            and overlay_geom.width() > 0
        )

    qtbot.waitUntil(_overlay_is_over_central, timeout=2000)

    # Final assertions (redundant but document the contract).
    overlay_geom = workspace.top_overlay().widget().geometry()
    assert overlay_geom.width() > 0, "TopOverlay must have a non-zero width"
    assert overlay_geom.height() > 0, "TopOverlay must have a non-zero height"

    central_top_left = central_widget.mapTo(workspace, central_widget.rect().topLeft())
    central_right = central_top_left.x() + central_widget.width()

    assert overlay_geom.x() >= central_top_left.x(), (
        "TopOverlay must not start before the Central Area"
    )
    assert overlay_geom.x() + overlay_geom.width() <= central_right, (
        "TopOverlay must not extend beyond the Central Area"
    )


def test_workspace_propagates_scene_query_to_editor(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    editor_registry: EditorRegistry,
    tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
    theme_service: ThemeService,
    project_context: ProjectContext,
) -> None:
    """The Editor receives the SceneQuery via its EditorContext."""
    scene_query = InMemorySceneQuery()
    workspace = Workspace(
        editor_registry=editor_registry,
        tool_registry=tool_registry,
        theme_service=theme_service,
        project_context=project_context,
        scene_query=scene_query,
    )
    qtbot.addWidget(workspace)

    spec = _spec(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.patient"))
    workspace.load_spec(spec)

    build_result = workspace._build_result
    assert build_result is not None
    editors = build_result.editors("area.central")
    assert len(editors) == 1
    # DummyEditor stores the context.
    context = editors[0]._context  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    assert context is not None
    assert context.scene_query is scene_query

======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/test_event_bus.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for EventBus."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import pytest

from cranioz.application.event_bus import EventBus, EventBusError


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SampleEvent:
    value: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OtherEvent:
    value: int


def test_publish_with_no_subscribers_is_noop() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    bus.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))  # must not raise


def test_subscribe_and_publish_receives_event() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    received: list[SampleEvent] = []
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, received.append)

    bus.publish(SampleEvent(value=42))

    assert received == [SampleEvent(value=42)]


def test_subscribers_are_isolated_by_event_type() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    received_sample: list[SampleEvent] = []
    received_other: list[OtherEvent] = []
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, received_sample.append)
    bus.subscribe(OtherEvent, received_other.append)

    bus.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))

    assert received_sample == [SampleEvent(value=1)]
    assert received_other == []


def test_multiple_subscribers_called_in_registration_order() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    order: list[str] = []
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: order.append("first"))
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: order.append("second"))
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: order.append("third"))

    bus.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))

    assert order == ["first", "second", "third"]


def test_unsubscribe_removes_handler() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    received: list[SampleEvent] = []
    handler = received.append
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, handler)
    bus.unsubscribe(SampleEvent, handler)

    bus.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))

    assert received == []


def test_unsubscribe_unknown_handler_is_noop() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    bus.unsubscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: None)  # must not raise


def test_failing_subscriber_does_not_block_others() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    called: list[str] = []

    def failing(_: SampleEvent) -> None:
        raise RuntimeError("boom")

    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, failing)
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: called.append("ok"))

    with pytest.raises(EventBusError):
        bus.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))

    assert called == ["ok"]


def test_subscriber_count() -> None:
    bus = EventBus()
    assert bus.subscriber_count(SampleEvent) == 0
    bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: None)
    assert bus.subscriber_count(SampleEvent) == 1
    assert bus.subscriber_count(OtherEvent) == 0



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/commands/test_base.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for the Command base types."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.application.commands.base import (
    Command,
    CommandDispatchError,
    CommandError,
    CommandNotRegistered,
)

# ----- Command ------------------------------------------------------


def test_command_new_generates_command_id() -> None:
    command = Command.new()
    assert isinstance(command.command_id, str)
    assert command.command_id != ""


def test_command_new_accepts_explicit_command_id() -> None:
    command = Command.new(command_id="test-1")
    assert command.command_id == "test-1"


def test_command_new_accepts_metadata() -> None:
    command = Command.new(metadata={"actor": "alice"})
    assert command.metadata == {"actor": "alice"}


def test_command_new_id_returns_unique_ids() -> None:
    a = Command.new_id()
    b = Command.new_id()
    assert a != b
    assert isinstance(a, str)
    assert isinstance(b, str)


def test_command_is_frozen() -> None:
    command = Command.new()
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        command.command_id = "other"  # type: ignore[misc]


def test_command_equality_by_value() -> None:
    a = Command.new(command_id="x")
    b = Command.new(command_id="x")
    assert a == b


def test_command_inequality_by_command_id() -> None:
    a = Command.new(command_id="x")
    b = Command.new(command_id="y")
    assert a != b


# ----- Errors ------------------------------------------------------


def test_command_error_hierarchy() -> None:
    assert issubclass(CommandNotRegistered, CommandError)
    assert issubclass(CommandDispatchError, CommandError)
    assert issubclass(CommandError, Exception)


def test_command_dispatch_error_carries_context() -> None:
    err = CommandDispatchError(
        "boom",
        command_id="c-1",
        command_type="MoveMaxillaCommand",
    )
    assert err.command_id == "c-1"
    assert err.command_type == "MoveMaxillaCommand"
    assert str(err) == "boom"


def test_command_dispatch_error_context_is_optional() -> None:
    err = CommandDispatchError("boom")
    assert err.command_id is None
    assert err.command_type is None


def test_command_dispatch_error_preserves_cause() -> None:
    original = ValueError("inner")
    try:
        try:
            raise original
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise CommandDispatchError("boom") from exc
    except CommandDispatchError as wrapped:
        assert wrapped.__cause__ is original



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/commands/test_executor.py
======================================================================

"""Contract tests for CommandExecutor."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import pytest

from cranioz.application.commands.base import (
    Command,
    CommandDispatchError,
    CommandNotRegistered,
)
from cranioz.application.commands.dispatcher import CommandDispatcher
from cranioz.application.commands.executor import CommandExecutor
from cranioz.application.commands.in_memory_bus import InMemoryCommandBus
from cranioz.application.commands.stack import InMemoryCommandStack
from cranioz.application.commands.transaction import (
    InMemoryTransactionManager,
)
from cranioz.application.event_bus import EventBus


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SampleCommand(Command):
    value: int = 0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CompensatingCommand(Command):
    value: int = 0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SampleEvent:
    value: int


class SampleHandler:
    """Handler that records calls and returns a result."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls: list[SampleCommand] = []

    def handle(self, command: SampleCommand) -> int:
        self.calls.append(command)
        return command.value * 2


class FailingHandler:
    """Handler that raises."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls: list[SampleCommand] = []

    def handle(self, command: SampleCommand) -> int:
        self.calls.append(command)
        raise RuntimeError("handler failed")


class EventPublishingHandler:
    """Handler that publishes an event (simulating a domain event)."""

    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus) -> None:
        self._event_bus = event_bus

    def handle(self, command: SampleCommand) -> int:
        self._event_bus.publish(SampleEvent(value=command.value))
        return command.value


@pytest.fixture
def event_bus() -> EventBus:
    return EventBus()


@pytest.fixture
def command_bus() -> InMemoryCommandBus:
    return InMemoryCommandBus()


@pytest.fixture
def command_stack() -> InMemoryCommandStack:
    return InMemoryCommandStack()


@pytest.fixture
def executor(
    command_bus: InMemoryCommandBus,
    command_stack: InMemoryCommandStack,
    event_bus: EventBus,
) -> CommandExecutor:
    return CommandExecutor(
        command_bus=command_bus,
        command_stack=command_stack,
        event_bus=event_bus,
        transaction_manager=InMemoryTransactionManager(),
    )


# ----- Protocol -----------------------------------------------------


def test_executor_satisfies_dispatcher_protocol(executor: CommandExecutor) -> None:
    assert isinstance(executor, CommandDispatcher)


# ----- Dispatch -----------------------------------------------------


def test_dispatch_calls_handler(
    executor: CommandExecutor,
    command_bus: InMemoryCommandBus,
) -> None:
    handler = SampleHandler()
    command_bus.register(SampleCommand, handler)

    result = executor.dispatch(SampleCommand(command_id="c-1", value=21))

    assert result == 42
    assert len(handler.calls) == 1
    assert handler.calls[0].value == 21


def test_dispatch_pushes_reversible_command_to_stack(
    executor: CommandExecutor,
    command_bus: InMemoryCommandBus,
) -> None:
    handler = SampleHandler()
    command_bus.register(SampleCommand, handler)

    executor.dispatch(SampleCommand(command_id="c-1"))

    assert executor.can_undo() is True
    assert executor.can_redo() is False


def test_dispatch_does_not_push_irreversible_command(
    executor: CommandExecutor,
    command_bus: InMemoryCommandBus,
) -> None:
    handler = SampleHandler()
    command_bus.register(SampleCommand, handler)

    executor.dispatch(SampleCommand(command_id="c-1", is_reversible=False))

    assert executor.can_undo() is False


def test_dispatch_unknown_raises(executor: CommandExecutor) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(CommandNotRegistered):
        executor.dispatch(SampleCommand(command_id="c-1"))


def test_dispatch_failing_handler_raises(
    executor: CommandExecutor,
    command_bus: InMemoryCommandBus,
) -> None:
    handler = FailingHandler()
    command_bus.register(SampleCommand, handler)

    with pytest.raises(CommandDispatchError):
        executor.dispatch(SampleCommand(command_id="c-1"))

    # No push on failure.
    assert executor.can_undo() is False


# ----- Event publication -------------------------------------------


def test_events_published_after_commit(
    executor: CommandExecutor,
    command_bus: InMemoryCommandBus,
    event_bus: EventBus,
) -> None:
    """Events must be published only after a successful commit."""
    received: list[SampleEvent] = []
    event_bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, received.append)

    handler = EventPublishingHandler(event_bus)
    command_bus.register(SampleCommand, handler)

    executor.dispatch(SampleCommand(command_id="c-1", value=42))

    assert received == [SampleEvent(value=42)]


def test_no_events_published_on_failure(
    executor: CommandExecutor,
    command_bus: InMemoryCommandBus,
    event_bus: EventBus,
) -> None:
    """A failing Handler must not publish events."""
    received: list[SampleEvent] = []
    event_bus.subscribe(SampleEvent, received.append)

    handler = FailingHandler()
    command_bus.register(SampleCommand, handler)

    with pytest.raises(CommandDispatchError):
        executor.dispatch(SampleCommand(command_id="c-1"))

    assert received == []


# ----- Undo/Redo ----------------------------------------------------


def test_undo_does_nothing_when_empty(executor: CommandExecutor) -> None:
    executor.undo()  # must not raise
    assert executor.can_undo() is False


def test_undo_dispatches_compensating(
    executor: CommandExecutor,
    command_bus: InMemoryCommandBus,
) -> None:
    handler = SampleHandler()
    command_bus.register(SampleCommand, handler)
    command_bus.register(CompensatingCommand, SampleHandler())

    compensating = CompensatingCommand(command_id="c-undo")
    command = SampleCommand(command_id="c-1", compensating=compensating)

    executor.dispatch(command)
    assert executor.can_undo() is True

    executor.undo()
    assert executor.can_undo() is False
    assert executor.can_redo() is True


def test_redo_dispatches_original(
    executor: CommandExecutor,
    command_bus: InMemoryCommandBus,
) -> None:
    handler = SampleHandler()
    command_bus.register(SampleCommand, handler)
    command_bus.register(CompensatingCommand, SampleHandler())

    compensating = CompensatingCommand(command_id="c-undo")
    command = SampleCommand(command_id="c-1", compensating=compensating)

    executor.dispatch(command)
    executor.undo()

    executor.redo()
    assert executor.can_undo() is True
    assert executor.can_redo() is False


def test_undo_unknown_compensating_raises(
    executor: CommandExecutor,
    command_bus: InMemoryCommandBus,
) -> None:
    handler = SampleHandler()
    command_bus.register(SampleCommand, handler)

    # CompensatingCommand is not registered.
    compensating = CompensatingCommand(command_id="c-undo")
    command = SampleCommand(command_id="c-1", compensating=compensating)

    executor.dispatch(command)
    with pytest.raises(CommandNotRegistered):
        executor.undo()



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/commands/test_in_memory_bus.py
======================================================================

"""Contract tests for CommandBus, exercised against InMemoryCommandBus.

Any future implementation of CommandBus (e.g., a CommandExecutor with
transaction boundaries and event publication) should pass these tests
unchanged, plus its own adapter-specific tests.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import pytest

from cranioz.application.commands.base import (
    Command,
    CommandDispatchError,
    CommandNotRegistered,
)
from cranioz.application.commands.bus import CommandBus
from cranioz.application.commands.in_memory_bus import InMemoryCommandBus

# ----- Fixtures -----------------------------------------------------


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SampleCommand(Command):
    value: int = 0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OtherCommand(Command):
    label: str = ""


class SampleHandler:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls: list[SampleCommand] = []

    def handle(self, command: SampleCommand) -> int:
        self.calls.append(command)
        return command.value * 2


class RaisingHandler:
    def handle(self, command: SampleCommand) -> int:
        raise RuntimeError("boom")


@pytest.fixture
def bus() -> InMemoryCommandBus:
    return InMemoryCommandBus()


# ----- Protocol -----------------------------------------------------


def test_bus_satisfies_protocol(bus: InMemoryCommandBus) -> None:
    assert isinstance(bus, CommandBus)


# ----- Dispatch -----------------------------------------------------


def test_dispatch_calls_handler_with_command(bus: InMemoryCommandBus) -> None:
    handler = SampleHandler()
    bus.register(SampleCommand, handler)
    result = bus.dispatch(SampleCommand(command_id="c-1", value=21))
    assert result == 42
    assert len(handler.calls) == 1
    assert handler.calls[0].value == 21


def test_dispatch_unknown_raises(bus: InMemoryCommandBus) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(CommandNotRegistered):
        bus.dispatch(SampleCommand(command_id="c-1", value=1))


def test_dispatch_unknown_error_mentions_type(bus: InMemoryCommandBus) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(CommandNotRegistered, match="SampleCommand"):
        bus.dispatch(SampleCommand(command_id="c-1"))


def test_dispatch_wraps_handler_error(bus: InMemoryCommandBus) -> None:
    bus.register(SampleCommand, RaisingHandler())
    with pytest.raises(CommandDispatchError) as exc_info:
        bus.dispatch(SampleCommand(command_id="c-1"))
    assert isinstance(exc_info.value.__cause__, RuntimeError)


def test_dispatch_error_carries_command_context(bus: InMemoryCommandBus) -> None:
    bus.register(SampleCommand, RaisingHandler())
    with pytest.raises(CommandDispatchError) as exc_info:
        bus.dispatch(SampleCommand(command_id="c-42"))
    err = exc_info.value
    assert err.command_id == "c-42"
    assert err.command_type == "SampleCommand"


# ----- Registration -------------------------------------------------


def test_register_different_types_is_allowed() -> None:
    bus = InMemoryCommandBus()
    bus.register(SampleCommand, SampleHandler())
    bus.register(OtherCommand, SampleHandler())
    assert bus.can_dispatch(SampleCommand)
    assert bus.can_dispatch(OtherCommand)


def test_register_same_type_same_handler_is_idempotent() -> None:
    bus = InMemoryCommandBus()
    handler = SampleHandler()
    bus.register(SampleCommand, handler)
    bus.register(SampleCommand, handler)
    assert bus.can_dispatch(SampleCommand)


def test_register_same_type_different_handler_raises() -> None:
    bus = InMemoryCommandBus()
    bus.register(SampleCommand, SampleHandler())
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="already registered"):
        bus.register(SampleCommand, SampleHandler())


def test_can_dispatch_reflects_registration() -> None:
    bus = InMemoryCommandBus()
    assert not bus.can_dispatch(SampleCommand)
    bus.register(SampleCommand, SampleHandler())
    assert bus.can_dispatch(SampleCommand)


def test_unregister_removes_handler() -> None:
    bus = InMemoryCommandBus()
    bus.register(SampleCommand, SampleHandler())
    bus.unregister(SampleCommand)
    assert not bus.can_dispatch(SampleCommand)


def test_unregister_unknown_is_noop() -> None:
    bus = InMemoryCommandBus()
    bus.unregister(SampleCommand)  # must not raise


def test_command_types_returns_registered_types() -> None:
    bus = InMemoryCommandBus()
    bus.register(SampleCommand, SampleHandler())
    bus.register(OtherCommand, SampleHandler())
    assert set(bus.command_types()) == {SampleCommand, OtherCommand}


def test_len_reflects_registration() -> None:
    bus = InMemoryCommandBus()
    assert len(bus) == 0
    bus.register(SampleCommand, SampleHandler())
    assert len(bus) == 1



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/commands/test_in_memory_stack.py
======================================================================

"""Contract tests for CommandStack, exercised against InMemoryCommandStack."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from cranioz.application.commands.base import Command
from cranioz.application.commands.stack import (
    CommandStack,
    InMemoryCommandStack,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SampleCommand(Command):
    tag: str = ""


def _cmd(tag: str) -> SampleCommand:
    return SampleCommand(command_id=Command.new_id(), tag=tag)


# ----- Protocol -----------------------------------------------------


def test_stack_satisfies_protocol() -> None:
    assert isinstance(InMemoryCommandStack(), CommandStack)


# ----- Empty stack --------------------------------------------------


def test_new_stack_is_empty() -> None:
    stack = InMemoryCommandStack()
    assert not stack.can_undo()
    assert not stack.can_redo()
    assert stack.undo() is None
    assert stack.redo() is None


# ----- Push / undo / redo -------------------------------------------


def test_push_then_undo_returns_compensating() -> None:
    stack = InMemoryCommandStack()
    original = _cmd("original")
    compensating = _cmd("compensating")
    stack.push(original, compensating)
    assert stack.can_undo()
    assert not stack.can_redo()
    assert stack.undo() is compensating
    assert stack.can_redo()
    assert not stack.can_undo()


def test_redo_returns_original() -> None:
    stack = InMemoryCommandStack()
    original = _cmd("original")
    compensating = _cmd("compensating")
    stack.push(original, compensating)
    stack.undo()
    assert stack.redo() is original
    assert stack.can_undo()
    assert not stack.can_redo()


def test_push_clears_redo() -> None:
    stack = InMemoryCommandStack()
    stack.push(_cmd("a"), _cmd("a-undo"))
    stack.undo()
    assert stack.can_redo()
    stack.push(_cmd("b"), _cmd("b-undo"))
    assert not stack.can_redo()


# ----- Irreversible Commands ---------------------------------------


def test_irreversible_command_pushes_with_none_compensating() -> None:
    stack = InMemoryCommandStack()
    stack.push(_cmd("irreversible"), None)
    assert stack.can_undo()
    assert stack.undo() is None
    assert stack.can_redo()


# ----- Multiple undo / redo ----------------------------------------


def test_multiple_undos_in_reverse_order() -> None:
    stack = InMemoryCommandStack()
    c1, k1 = _cmd("c1"), _cmd("k1")
    c2, k2 = _cmd("c2"), _cmd("k2")
    stack.push(c1, k1)
    stack.push(c2, k2)
    assert stack.undo() is k2
    assert stack.undo() is k1
    assert stack.undo() is None


def test_multiple_redos_in_forward_order() -> None:
    stack = InMemoryCommandStack()
    c1, k1 = _cmd("c1"), _cmd("k1")
    c2, k2 = _cmd("c2"), _cmd("k2")
    stack.push(c1, k1)
    stack.push(c2, k2)
    stack.undo()
    stack.undo()
    assert stack.redo() is c1
    assert stack.redo() is c2
    assert stack.redo() is None


# ----- Clear --------------------------------------------------------


def test_clear_empties_both_stacks() -> None:
    stack = InMemoryCommandStack()
    stack.push(_cmd("a"), _cmd("a-undo"))
    stack.undo()
    stack.clear()
    assert not stack.can_undo()
    assert not stack.can_redo()

"""Tests for InMemoryTransactionManager."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.application.commands.transaction import (
    InMemoryTransactionManager,
    TransactionManager,
)


def test_satisfies_protocol() -> None:
    assert isinstance(InMemoryTransactionManager(), TransactionManager)


def test_commit_on_success() -> None:
    tm = InMemoryTransactionManager()
    with tm.begin():
        pass  # no exception → commit


def test_rollback_on_exception() -> None:
    tm = InMemoryTransactionManager()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError), tm.begin():
        raise ValueError("boom")

======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/events/test_bus_adapter.py

"""Contract tests for EventSubscription and EventPublisher.

Exercised against ``EventBusAdapter`` wrapping the real ``EventBus``.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

import pytest

from cranioz.application.event_bus import EventBus
from cranioz.application.events import (
    EventBusAdapter,
    EventPublisher,
    EventSubscription,
    SubscriptionClosed,
    SubscriptionHandle,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SampleEvent:
    value: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OtherEvent:
    label: str


@pytest.fixture
def bus() -> EventBus:
    return EventBus()


@pytest.fixture
def adapter(bus: EventBus) -> EventBusAdapter:
    return EventBusAdapter(bus)


# ----- Protocol conformance ----------------------------------------


def test_subscription_satisfies_protocol(adapter: EventBusAdapter) -> None:
    sub = adapter.create_subscription()
    assert isinstance(sub, EventSubscription)


def test_publisher_satisfies_protocol(adapter: EventBusAdapter) -> None:
    pub = adapter.create_publisher()
    assert isinstance(pub, EventPublisher)


# ----- Subscription basics -----------------------------------------


def test_subscribe_then_publish_calls_handler(
    adapter: EventBusAdapter,
) -> None:
    received: list[SampleEvent] = []
    sub = adapter.create_subscription()
    handle = sub.subscribe(SampleEvent, received.append)
    assert isinstance(handle, SubscriptionHandle)

    pub = adapter.create_publisher()
    pub.publish(SampleEvent(value=42))

    assert received == [SampleEvent(value=42)]


def test_publish_isolated_by_event_type(
    adapter: EventBusAdapter,
) -> None:
    samples: list[SampleEvent] = []
    others: list[OtherEvent] = []
    sub = adapter.create_subscription()
    sub.subscribe(SampleEvent, samples.append)
    sub.subscribe(OtherEvent, others.append)

    pub = adapter.create_publisher()
    pub.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))

    assert samples == [SampleEvent(value=1)]
    assert others == []


# ----- Multiple handles in one scope --------------------------------


def test_scope_tracks_multiple_handles(adapter: EventBusAdapter) -> None:
    sub = adapter.create_subscription()
    sub.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: None)
    sub.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: None)
    assert len(sub) == 2


def test_dispose_cancels_all_handles(adapter: EventBusAdapter) -> None:
    received: list[SampleEvent] = []
    sub = adapter.create_subscription()
    sub.subscribe(SampleEvent, received.append)

    sub.dispose()

    pub = adapter.create_publisher()
    pub.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))
    assert received == []


def test_dispose_is_idempotent(adapter: EventBusAdapter) -> None:
    sub = adapter.create_subscription()
    sub.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: None)
    sub.dispose()
    sub.dispose()  # must not raise


def test_subscribe_after_dispose_raises(
    adapter: EventBusAdapter,
) -> None:
    sub = adapter.create_subscription()
    sub.dispose()
    with pytest.raises(SubscriptionClosed):
        sub.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: None)


# ----- Individual handle cancellation ------------------------------


def test_handle_cancel_removes_only_that_handler(
    adapter: EventBusAdapter,
) -> None:
    a: list[SampleEvent] = []
    b: list[SampleEvent] = []
    sub = adapter.create_subscription()
    h_a = sub.subscribe(SampleEvent, a.append)
    sub.subscribe(SampleEvent, b.append)

    h_a.cancel()

    pub = adapter.create_publisher()
    pub.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))
    assert a == []
    assert b == [SampleEvent(value=1)]


def test_handle_cancel_is_idempotent(adapter: EventBusAdapter) -> None:
    sub = adapter.create_subscription()
    h = sub.subscribe(SampleEvent, lambda _: None)
    h.cancel()
    h.cancel()  # must not raise


# ----- Independent scopes ------------------------------------------


def test_two_scopes_are_independent(adapter: EventBusAdapter) -> None:
    a: list[SampleEvent] = []
    b: list[SampleEvent] = []
    sub_a = adapter.create_subscription()
    sub_b = adapter.create_subscription()
    sub_a.subscribe(SampleEvent, a.append)
    sub_b.subscribe(SampleEvent, b.append)

    sub_a.dispose()

    pub = adapter.create_publisher()
    pub.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))
    assert a == []
    assert b == [SampleEvent(value=1)]


# ----- Publisher error handling -----------------------------------


def test_publisher_logs_subscriber_failure_without_raising(
    adapter: EventBusAdapter,
    caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture,
) -> None:
    def failing(_: SampleEvent) -> None:
        raise RuntimeError("boom")

    sub = adapter.create_subscription()
    sub.subscribe(SampleEvent, failing)

    pub = adapter.create_publisher()
    # Must not raise; logs the failure.
    pub.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))


# ----- Subscription disposal is not automatic on publisher side ----


def test_publisher_does_not_own_subscriptions(
    adapter: EventBusAdapter,
) -> None:
    received: list[SampleEvent] = []
    sub = adapter.create_subscription()
    sub.subscribe(SampleEvent, received.append)

    pub = adapter.create_publisher()
    pub.publish(SampleEvent(value=1))
    pub.publish(SampleEvent(value=2))

    assert received == [SampleEvent(value=1), SampleEvent(value=2)]

======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/flows/test_catalog.py

"""Tests for FlowCatalog and InMemoryFlowRepository."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.application.flows.catalog import FlowCatalog
from cranioz.application.flows.definition import (
    FlowDefinition,
    FlowStep,
)
from cranioz.application.flows.errors import (
    FlowCorrupted,
    FlowNotFound,
)
from cranioz.application.flows.in_memory_repository import (
    InMemoryFlowRepository,
)
from cranioz.application.flows.repository import FlowRepository


def _step(step_id: str = "s1") -> FlowStep:
    return FlowStep(step_id=step_id, name_key=f"steps.{step_id}", module_id="m")


def _flow(flow_id: str, steps: tuple[FlowStep, ...] = (_step(),)) -> FlowDefinition:
    return FlowDefinition(
        flow_id=flow_id,
        name_key=f"flows.{flow_id}",
        version=1,
        steps=steps,
    )


@pytest.fixture
def repository() -> InMemoryFlowRepository:
    return InMemoryFlowRepository()


@pytest.fixture
def catalog(repository: InMemoryFlowRepository) -> FlowCatalog:
    return FlowCatalog(repository=repository)


def test_repository_satisfies_protocol(
    repository: InMemoryFlowRepository,
) -> None:
    assert isinstance(repository, FlowRepository)


def test_empty_catalog(catalog: FlowCatalog) -> None:
    assert catalog.list_flows() == ()
    assert catalog.count() == 0
    assert catalog.is_empty() is True


def test_add_then_list(repository: InMemoryFlowRepository) -> None:
    repository.add(_flow("orthognathic"))
    catalog = FlowCatalog(repository=repository)
    summaries = catalog.list_flows()
    assert len(summaries) == 1
    assert summaries[0].flow_id == "orthognathic"
    assert summaries[0].step_count == 1


def test_get_flow(repository: InMemoryFlowRepository) -> None:
    repository.add(_flow("orthognathic"))
    catalog = FlowCatalog(repository=repository)
    flow = catalog.get_flow("orthognathic")
    assert flow.flow_id == "orthognathic"


def test_get_flow_missing_raises(catalog: FlowCatalog) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(FlowNotFound):
        catalog.get_flow("unknown")


def test_get_flow_summary_returns_none(catalog: FlowCatalog) -> None:
    assert catalog.get_flow_summary("unknown") is None


def test_contains(repository: InMemoryFlowRepository) -> None:
    repository.add(_flow("orthognathic"))
    catalog = FlowCatalog(repository=repository)
    assert catalog.contains("orthognathic") is True
    assert catalog.contains("unknown") is False


def test_search_empty_returns_all(
    repository: InMemoryFlowRepository,
) -> None:
    repository.add(_flow("a"))
    repository.add(_flow("b"))
    catalog = FlowCatalog(repository=repository)
    assert len(catalog.search_flows("")) == 2
    assert len(catalog.search_flows("   ")) == 2


def test_search_matches_name_key(
    repository: InMemoryFlowRepository,
) -> None:
    repository.add(_flow("orthognathic"))
    repository.add(_flow("implants"))
    catalog = FlowCatalog(repository=repository)
    result = catalog.search_flows("ortho")
    assert len(result) == 1
    assert result[0].flow_id == "orthognathic"


def test_corrupted_flow_is_invisible(
    repository: InMemoryFlowRepository,
) -> None:
    repository.add(_flow("ok"))
    repository.add(_flow("bad"))
    repository._mark_corrupted("bad")
    catalog = FlowCatalog(repository=repository)
    assert catalog.count() == 1
    with pytest.raises(FlowCorrupted):
        catalog.get_flow("bad")


def test_list_definitions(repository: InMemoryFlowRepository) -> None:
    repository.add(_flow("a"))
    repository.add(_flow("b"))
    catalog = FlowCatalog(repository=repository)
    definitions = catalog.list_definitions()
    assert {d.flow_id for d in definitions} == {"a", "b"}



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/flows/test_definition.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for FlowDefinition and FlowStep."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.application.flows.definition import (
    FlowDefinition,
    FlowStep,
)


def _step(step_id: str = "s1", module_id: str = "m1") -> FlowStep:
    return FlowStep(
        step_id=step_id,
        name_key=f"steps.{step_id}",
        module_id=module_id,
    )


def _flow(
    flow_id: str = "f1",
    steps: tuple[FlowStep, ...] = (_step(),),
) -> FlowDefinition:
    return FlowDefinition(
        flow_id=flow_id,
        name_key=f"flows.{flow_id}",
        version=1,
        steps=steps,
    )


def test_step_holds_fields() -> None:
    s = _step("patient", "patient_module")
    assert s.step_id == "patient"
    assert s.name_key == "steps.patient"
    assert s.module_id == "patient_module"


def test_step_is_frozen() -> None:
    s = _step()
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        s.step_id = "x"  # type: ignore[misc]


def test_empty_step_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="step_id"):
        FlowStep(step_id="", name_key="x", module_id="m")


def test_empty_name_key_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="name_key"):
        FlowStep(step_id="s", name_key="", module_id="m")


def test_empty_module_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="module_id"):
        FlowStep(step_id="s", name_key="k", module_id="")


def test_flow_holds_fields() -> None:
    f = _flow()
    assert f.flow_id == "f1"
    assert f.name_key == "flows.f1"
    assert f.version == 1
    assert len(f.steps) == 1


def test_flow_is_frozen() -> None:
    f = _flow()
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        f.flow_id = "x"  # type: ignore[misc]


def test_empty_flow_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="flow_id"):
        FlowDefinition(flow_id="", name_key="k", version=1, steps=(_step(),))


def test_version_zero_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="version"):
        FlowDefinition(flow_id="f", name_key="k", version=0, steps=(_step(),))


def test_empty_steps_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="steps must not be empty"):
        FlowDefinition(flow_id="f", name_key="k", version=1, steps=())


def test_duplicate_step_ids_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unique"):
        FlowDefinition(
            flow_id="f",
            name_key="k",
            version=1,
            steps=(_step("s1"), _step("s1")),
        )


def test_step_count() -> None:
    f = _flow(steps=(_step("s1"), _step("s2"), _step("s3")))
    assert f.step_count() == 3

FILE: tests/unit/application/imaging/test_import_report.py

"""Tests for ImportReport and ImportIssue."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.application.imaging.import_report import (
    ImportIssue,
    ImportReport,
    IssueSeverity,
)


def _issue(severity: IssueSeverity, code: str = "test", message: str = "msg") -> ImportIssue:
    return ImportIssue(severity=severity, code=code, message=message)


def test_issue_holds_fields() -> None:
    issue = _issue(IssueSeverity.ERROR, code="x", message="y")
    assert issue.severity is IssueSeverity.ERROR
    assert issue.code == "x"
    assert issue.message == "y"


def test_issue_empty_code_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="code"):
        _issue(IssueSeverity.ERROR, code="")


def test_issue_empty_message_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="message"):
        _issue(IssueSeverity.ERROR, message="")


def test_report_has_errors() -> None:
    report = ImportReport(
        success=False,
        files_detected=10,
        instances_loaded=0,
        instances_skipped=10,
        modality="CT",
        transfer_syntax=None,
        dimensions=None,
        spacing=None,
        intensity_scale=None,
        issues=(_issue(IssueSeverity.ERROR),),
    )
    assert report.has_errors is True
    assert report.has_warnings is False


def test_report_has_warnings() -> None:
    report = ImportReport(
        success=True,
        files_detected=10,
        instances_loaded=10,
        instances_skipped=0,
        modality="CT",
        transfer_syntax=None,
        dimensions=(10, 10, 10),
        spacing=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0),
        intensity_scale="hu",
        issues=(_issue(IssueSeverity.WARNING),),
    )
    assert report.has_errors is False
    assert report.has_warnings is True


def test_issues_by_severity() -> None:
    issues = (
        _issue(IssueSeverity.ERROR, code="e1"),
        _issue(IssueSeverity.WARNING, code="w1"),
        _issue(IssueSeverity.WARNING, code="w2"),
        _issue(IssueSeverity.INFO, code="i1"),
    )
    report = ImportReport(
        success=False,
        files_detected=1,
        instances_loaded=1,
        instances_skipped=0,
        modality="CT",
        transfer_syntax=None,
        dimensions=None,
        spacing=None,
        intensity_scale=None,
        issues=issues,
    )
    assert len(report.issues_by_severity(IssueSeverity.ERROR)) == 1
    assert len(report.issues_by_severity(IssueSeverity.WARNING)) == 2
    assert len(report.issues_by_severity(IssueSeverity.INFO)) == 1


def test_summary_contains_key_fields() -> None:
    report = ImportReport(
        success=True,
        files_detected=3,
        instances_loaded=3,
        instances_skipped=0,
        modality="CT",
        transfer_syntax=None,
        dimensions=(10, 10, 10),
        spacing=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0),
        intensity_scale="hu",
        issues=(),
    )
    text = report.summary()
    assert "files=3" in text
    assert "modality=CT" in text
    assert "scale=hu" in text

======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/project/test_filesystem_project_repository.py
======================================================================

"""FilesystemProjectRepository — contract + filesystem-specific tests.

This file runs the *same contract* defined in Peça 7.1 against the
filesystem adapter. If the adapter honors the contract, the same test
bodies that pass for `InMemoryProjectRepository` must pass here.

Additional tests cover filesystem-specific behavior:

- `create` leaves no partial directory when project.json cannot be
  written.
- `exists` is keyed on `project.json`, not the directory (so a stray
  directory without project.json is not a project).
- `delete` removes only `project.json`; other files in the directory
  are left untouched (they belong to other persistence layers).
- `list_metadata` skips corrupted entries (a malformed project.json
  in one directory does not break the listing).
- `list_ids` skips non-project directories.

Qt-free, I/O is real but confined to `tmp_path`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from cranioz.application.project.project_errors import (
    ProjectAlreadyExists,
    ProjectCorrupted,
    ProjectNotFound,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_repository import ProjectRepository
from cranioz.domain.project.project import Project
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus
from cranioz.domain.project.project_summary import ProjectSummary
from cranioz.infrastructure.project.filesystem_project_repository import (
    FilesystemProjectRepository,
)
from cranioz.infrastructure.project.project_serialization import (
    CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    project_to_dict,
)

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def _metadata(project_id: str = "p-001") -> ProjectMetadata:
    return ProjectMetadata(
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
        project_name=f"Case {project_id}",
        patient_id="pat-001",
        patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
        status=ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
        created_at=_now(),
        updated_at=_now(),
        last_opened_at=None,
        schema_version=CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    )


def _project(project_id: str = "p-001") -> Project:
    pid = ProjectId(project_id)
    return Project(project_id=pid, metadata=_metadata(project_id))


@pytest.fixture
def root(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:
    """Isolated projects root for the test."""
    projects = tmp_path / "projects"
    projects.mkdir()
    return projects


@pytest.fixture
def repo(root: Path) -> ProjectRepository:
    return FilesystemProjectRepository(projects_root=root)


# ====================================================================
# Contract — same bodies as Peça 7.1
# ====================================================================


def test_satisfies_protocol(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    assert isinstance(repo, ProjectRepository)


def test_new_repository_is_empty(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    assert repo.list_metadata() == ()
    assert repo.list_ids() == ()


def test_create_then_get_returns_project(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    project = _project("p-001")
    repo.create(project)
    loaded = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert loaded.project_id == project.project_id
    assert loaded.metadata == project.metadata


def test_create_then_exists_is_true(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-001")) is True


def test_exists_false_for_missing_project(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-999")) is False


def test_get_missing_raises_project_not_found(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound) as exc_info:
        repo.get(ProjectId("p-999"))
    assert exc_info.value.project_id == ProjectId("p-999")


def test_create_duplicate_raises_project_already_exists(
    repo: ProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    with pytest.raises(ProjectAlreadyExists) as exc_info:
        repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert exc_info.value.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")


def test_create_duplicate_does_not_overwrite(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    first = _project("p-001")
    repo.create(first)
    with pytest.raises(ProjectAlreadyExists):
        repo.create(_project("p-001"))

    loaded = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert loaded.metadata == first.metadata


def test_update_replaces_stored_instance(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    original = _project("p-001")
    repo.create(original)

    later = datetime(2026, 2, 1, tzinfo=UTC)
    updated = Project(
        project_id=original.project_id,
        metadata=original.metadata.with_name("Renamed", now=later),
    )
    repo.update(updated)

    loaded = repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert loaded.metadata.project_name == "Renamed"


def test_update_missing_raises_project_not_found(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound):
        repo.update(_project("p-999"))


def test_delete_removes_project(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.delete(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("p-001")) is False
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound):
        repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))


def test_delete_missing_raises_project_not_found(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ProjectNotFound):
        repo.delete(ProjectId("p-999"))


def test_list_metadata_returns_summaries(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.create(_project("p-002"))

    result = repo.list_metadata()
    assert len(result) == 2
    for item in result:
        assert isinstance(item, ProjectSummary)


def test_list_metadata_reflects_current_state(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.create(_project("p-002"))

    assert {s.project_id for s in repo.list_metadata()} == {
        ProjectId("p-001"),
        ProjectId("p-002"),
    }

    repo.delete(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert {s.project_id for s in repo.list_metadata()} == {ProjectId("p-002")}


def test_list_ids_matches_created_ids(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.create(_project("p-002"))
    assert set(repo.list_ids()) == {ProjectId("p-001"), ProjectId("p-002")}


def test_list_ids_empty_when_nothing_created(repo: ProjectRepository) -> None:
    assert repo.list_ids() == ()


# ====================================================================
# Filesystem-specific behavior
# ====================================================================


def test_create_writes_project_json(repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path) -> None:
    project = _project("p-001")
    repo.create(project)

    project_json = root / "p-001" / "project.json"
    assert project_json.is_file()
    data = json.loads(project_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert data == project_to_dict(project)


def test_create_does_not_write_patient_record(repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path) -> None:
    """patient_record.json belongs to the Patient persistence layer."""
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert not (root / "p-001" / "patient_record.json").exists()


def test_exists_is_false_for_directory_without_project_json(
    repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path
) -> None:
    """A stray directory is not a project.

    This can happen if a crash leaves a directory behind, or if
    another layer (e.g. patient persistence) created it first.
    """
    (root / "stray").mkdir()
    assert repo.exists(ProjectId("stray")) is False


def test_delete_removes_only_project_json(repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path) -> None:
    """Other files in the directory belong to other layers."""
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    # Simulate a patient record written by another layer.
    patient_record = root / "p-001" / "patient_record.json"
    patient_record.write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")

    repo.delete(ProjectId("p-001"))

    assert not (root / "p-001" / "project.json").exists()
    assert patient_record.is_file()  # untouched


def test_list_ids_skips_non_project_directories(repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    (root / "stray").mkdir()  # no project.json

    assert repo.list_ids() == (ProjectId("p-001"),)


def test_list_ids_skips_non_directory_entries(repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    (root / "file.txt").write_text("x", encoding="utf-8")

    assert repo.list_ids() == (ProjectId("p-001"),)


def test_list_ids_includes_all_project_directories(repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path) -> None:
    """Any directory with a project.json is a project, regardless of name.

    The `ProjectId` value object itself does not reject path-unsafe
    names; that validation belongs to `ProjectPathResolver` at the
    point of use. `list_ids` reports what is on disk.
    """
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    repo.create(_project("weird.name"))

    ids = set(repo.list_ids())
    assert ProjectId("p-001") in ids
    assert ProjectId("weird.name") in ids


def test_get_corrupted_json_raises_project_corrupted(repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    (root / "p-001" / "project.json").write_text("{not-json", encoding="utf-8")

    with pytest.raises(ProjectCorrupted):
        repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))


def test_get_non_object_json_raises_project_corrupted(repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    (root / "p-001" / "project.json").write_text("[1, 2, 3]", encoding="utf-8")

    with pytest.raises(ProjectCorrupted):
        repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))


def test_get_schema_mismatch_raises_project_corrupted(repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path) -> None:
    repo.create(_project("p-001"))
    project_json = root / "p-001" / "project.json"
    data = json.loads(project_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    data["schema_version"] = 99
    project_json.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")

    with pytest.raises(ProjectCorrupted):
        repo.get(ProjectId("p-001"))


def test_list_metadata_skips_corrupted_entries(repo: ProjectRepository, root: Path) -> None:
    """One corrupted project does not break the listing."""

"""Tests for ProjectAccessPolicy and AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy."""

from __future__ import annotations

from cranioz.application.project.project_access_policy import (
    AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy,
    ProjectAccessPolicy,
)
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId


def test_allow_all_satisfies_protocol() -> None:
    policy = AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy()
    assert isinstance(policy, ProjectAccessPolicy)


def test_allow_all_permits_create() -> None:
    policy = AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy()
    assert policy.can_create("alice") is True


def test_allow_all_permits_open() -> None:
    policy = AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy()
    assert policy.can_open("alice", ProjectId("p-001")) is True


def test_allow_all_permits_update() -> None:
    policy = AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy()
    assert policy.can_update("alice", ProjectId("p-001")) is True


def test_allow_all_permits_delete() -> None:
    policy = AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy()
    assert policy.can_delete("alice", ProjectId("p-001")) is True


def test_allow_all_ignores_actor() -> None:
    """The permissive policy is actor-agnostic."""
    policy = AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy()
    assert policy.can_create("") is True
    assert policy.can_open("", ProjectId("p-001")) is True



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/project/test_project_catalog.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for ProjectCatalog.

The catalog is a thin read-only projection over a ProjectRepository.
Tests exercise it against InMemoryProjectRepository — pure Python, no
I/O, no Qt.

Coverage:

- `list_projects` delegates to `repository.list_metadata`.
- `get_project_summary` returns the summary when present, None when
  not. It does not raise `ProjectNotFound`.
- `search_projects` is case-insensitive substring over project name
  and patient display name. Empty query returns all.
- `count`, `is_empty`, `contains` reflect the repository state.
- The catalog does not cache: mutations to the repository are
  reflected on the next call.
- A corrupted entry in the repository is invisible to the catalog
  (because `list_metadata` skips corrupted entries).
- The catalog does not expose lifecycle methods (create, delete, ...).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest

from cranioz.application.project.in_memory_project_repository import (
    InMemoryProjectRepository,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_catalog import ProjectCatalog
from cranioz.domain.project.project import Project
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus
from cranioz.domain.project.project_summary import ProjectSummary

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def _project(
    project_id: str,
    *,
    project_name: str | None = None,
    patient_display_name: str = "Gabriel Silva",
) -> Project:
    pid = ProjectId(project_id)
    return Project(
        project_id=pid,
        metadata=ProjectMetadata(
            project_id=pid,
            project_name=project_name or f"Case {project_id}",
            patient_id="pat-001",
            patient_display_name=patient_display_name,
            clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
            status=ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
            created_at=_now(),
            updated_at=_now(),
            last_opened_at=None,
            schema_version=1,
        ),
    )


@pytest.fixture
def repository() -> InMemoryProjectRepository:
    return InMemoryProjectRepository()


@pytest.fixture
def catalog(repository: InMemoryProjectRepository) -> ProjectCatalog:
    return ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)


# ----- Construction -------------------------------------------------


def test_catalog_holds_repository(repository: InMemoryProjectRepository) -> None:
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)
    # Sanity: constructing does not raise; the catalog is usable.
    assert catalog.list_projects() == ()


# ----- list_projects ------------------------------------------------


def test_list_projects_empty(catalog: ProjectCatalog) -> None:
    assert catalog.list_projects() == ()


def test_list_projects_returns_all(repository: InMemoryProjectRepository) -> None:
    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    repository.create(_project("p-002"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    result = catalog.list_projects()
    assert len(result) == 2
    for item in result:
        assert isinstance(item, ProjectSummary)


def test_list_projects_delegates_to_repository(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    """The catalog must not maintain its own list.

    If it did, mutations after the first call would not be visible.
    """
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert len(catalog.list_projects()) == 1

    repository.create(_project("p-002"))
    assert len(catalog.list_projects()) == 2


def test_list_projects_reflects_deletion(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    repository.create(_project("p-002"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    repository.delete(ProjectId("p-001"))
    ids = {s.project_id for s in catalog.list_projects()}
    assert ids == {ProjectId("p-002")}


# ----- get_project_summary -----------------------------------------


def test_get_project_summary_returns_summary(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repository.create(_project("p-001", project_name="Alpha"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    summary = catalog.get_project_summary(ProjectId("p-001"))
    assert summary is not None
    assert summary.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert summary.project_name == "Alpha"


def test_get_project_summary_returns_none_for_missing(
    catalog: ProjectCatalog,
) -> None:
    """The catalog returns None, it does not raise."""
    assert catalog.get_project_summary(ProjectId("p-999")) is None


def test_get_project_summary_does_not_raise_for_missing(
    catalog: ProjectCatalog,
) -> None:
    """Explicit: no exception escapes get_project_summary for a missing id."""
    try:
        catalog.get_project_summary(ProjectId("p-999"))
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        pytest.fail(f"unexpected exception: {exc!r}")


# ----- search_projects ---------------------------------------------


def test_search_empty_query_returns_all(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    repository.create(_project("p-002"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    assert len(catalog.search_projects("")) == 2
    assert len(catalog.search_projects("   ")) == 2


def test_search_matches_project_name_case_insensitive(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repository.create(_project("p-001", project_name="Alpha Case"))
    repository.create(_project("p-002", project_name="Beta Case"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    result = catalog.search_projects("alpha")
    assert len(result) == 1
    assert result[0].project_name == "Alpha Case"

    result = catalog.search_projects("ALPHA")
    assert len(result) == 1


def test_search_matches_patient_display_name(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    repository.create(_project("p-001", project_name="A", patient_display_name="Maria Souza"))
    repository.create(_project("p-002", project_name="B", patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    result = catalog.search_projects("maria")
    assert len(result) == 1
    assert result[0].patient_display_name == "Maria Souza"


def test_search_substring_matches(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    """Substring, not prefix. 'ognath' matches 'Orthognathic'."""
    repository.create(_project("p-001", project_name="Orthognathic Case"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    assert len(catalog.search_projects("ognath")) == 1


def test_search_no_match_returns_empty(
    catalog: ProjectCatalog,
) -> None:
    assert catalog.search_projects("zzz") == ()


def test_search_does_not_use_regex(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    """A regex-looking query is treated literally.

    If the catalog used regex, '.' would match any character. Here it
    must match only a literal dot. The project name contains 'a.b',
    so 'a.b' matches but 'axb' does not.
    """
    repository.create(_project("p-001", project_name="a.b"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    assert len(catalog.search_projects("a.b")) == 1
    assert len(catalog.search_projects("axb")) == 0


# ----- count / is_empty / contains ---------------------------------


def test_count_reflects_repository(catalog: ProjectCatalog, repository) -> None:
    assert catalog.count() == 0
    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert catalog.count() == 1
    repository.create(_project("p-002"))
    assert catalog.count() == 2


def test_is_empty_reflects_repository(catalog: ProjectCatalog, repository) -> None:
    assert catalog.is_empty() is True
    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert catalog.is_empty() is False


def test_contains_true_for_existing(catalog: ProjectCatalog, repository) -> None:
    repository.create(_project("p-001"))
    assert catalog.contains(ProjectId("p-001")) is True


def test_contains_false_for_missing(catalog: ProjectCatalog) -> None:
    assert catalog.contains(ProjectId("p-999")) is False


# ----- Corrupted entries -------------------------------------------


def test_corrupted_entries_are_invisible(
    repository: InMemoryProjectRepository,
) -> None:
    """The catalog relies on list_metadata, which skips corrupted entries."""
    repository.create(_project("p-ok"))
    repository.create(_project("p-bad"))
    repository._mark_corrupted(ProjectId("p-bad"))
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repository)

    assert catalog.count() == 1
    assert catalog.contains(ProjectId("p-ok")) is True
    assert catalog.contains(ProjectId("p-bad")) is False


# ----- No lifecycle methods ----------------------------------------


def test_catalog_has_no_create_method(catalog: ProjectCatalog) -> None:
    """The catalog is read-only. It must not expose create()."""
    assert not hasattr(catalog, "create")


def test_catalog_has_no_update_method(catalog: ProjectCatalog) -> None:
    assert not hasattr(catalog, "update")


def test_catalog_has_no_delete_method(catalog: ProjectCatalog) -> None:
    assert not hasattr(catalog, "delete")


def test_catalog_has_no_open_method(catalog: ProjectCatalog) -> None:
    assert not hasattr(catalog, "open_project")



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/application/project/test_project_context.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for ProjectContext and ObjectReference.

The ProjectContext is the immutable snapshot handed to the Workspace
when a project opens. These tests cover:

- Construction and required/optional fields.
- The metadata/project_id coherence invariant.
- The workflow_id/workflow_config coupling invariant.
- Immutability (frozen).
- Functional mutators (`with_workflow`, `with_object_references`).
- `has_workflow` helper.
- `ObjectReference` validation.

No Qt, no I/O, no repository.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
from datetime import UTC, datetime

import pytest

from cranioz.application.project.project_context import (
    ObjectReference,
    ProjectContext,
)
from cranioz.domain.patient.enums.patient_sex import PatientSex
from cranioz.domain.patient.patient_summary import PatientSummary
from cranioz.domain.patient.value_objects.patient_id import PatientId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_metadata import ProjectMetadata
from cranioz.domain.project.project_status import ProjectStatus

# ----- Helpers ------------------------------------------------------


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def _metadata(project_id: str = "p-001") -> ProjectMetadata:
    return ProjectMetadata(
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
        project_name=f"Case {project_id}",
        patient_id="pat-001",
        patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
        status=ProjectStatus.ACTIVE,
        created_at=_now(),
        updated_at=_now(),
        last_opened_at=None,
        schema_version=1,
    )


def _patient_summary() -> PatientSummary:
    return PatientSummary(
        patient_id=PatientId("pat-001"),
        display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        birth_date=None,
        sex=PatientSex.MALE,
    )


def _context(
    project_id: str = "p-001",
    *,
    workflow_id: str | None = None,
    workflow_config: dict | None = None,
    object_references: tuple[ObjectReference, ...] = (),
) -> ProjectContext:
    return ProjectContext(
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
        metadata=_metadata(project_id),
        patient_summary=_patient_summary(),
        workflow_id=workflow_id,
        workflow_config=workflow_config,
        object_references=object_references,
    )


# ----- ObjectReference ---------------------------------------------


def test_object_reference_holds_fields() -> None:
    ref = ObjectReference(
        object_id="ct-001",
        kind="volume",
        display_name="CT Pre-op",
    )
    assert ref.object_id == "ct-001"
    assert ref.kind == "volume"
    assert ref.display_name == "CT Pre-op"


def test_object_reference_is_frozen() -> None:
    ref = ObjectReference(object_id="x", kind="k", display_name="d")
    attr = "kind"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(ref, attr, "other")


def test_object_reference_rejects_empty_object_id() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="object_id"):
        ObjectReference(object_id="", kind="k", display_name="d")


def test_object_reference_rejects_empty_kind() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="kind"):
        ObjectReference(object_id="x", kind="", display_name="d")


def test_object_reference_rejects_empty_display_name() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="display_name"):
        ObjectReference(object_id="x", kind="k", display_name="")


# ----- ProjectContext construction ---------------------------------


def test_context_holds_all_fields() -> None:
    ref = ObjectReference(object_id="ct-001", kind="volume", display_name="CT")
    ctx = _context(object_references=(ref,))

    assert ctx.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert ctx.metadata.project_id == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert ctx.patient_summary.display_name == "Gabriel Silva"
    assert ctx.workflow_id is None
    assert ctx.workflow_config is None
    assert ctx.object_references == (ref,)


def test_context_defaults_workflow_to_none() -> None:
    ctx = _context()
    assert ctx.workflow_id is None
    assert ctx.workflow_config is None
    assert ctx.has_workflow() is False


def test_context_accepts_workflow_when_both_are_set() -> None:
    ctx = _context(
        workflow_id="orthognathic_planning",
        workflow_config={"steps": ["patient_data"]},
    )
    assert ctx.workflow_id == "orthognathic_planning"
    assert ctx.workflow_config == {"steps": ["patient_data"]}
    assert ctx.has_workflow() is True


def test_context_is_frozen() -> None:
    ctx = _context()
    attr = "project_id"
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(ctx, attr, ProjectId("p-999"))


def test_context_object_references_default_is_empty_tuple() -> None:
    ctx = _context()
    assert ctx.object_references == ()


def test_context_object_references_default_is_immutable_tuple() -> None:
    ctx = _context()
    assert isinstance(ctx.object_references, tuple)


# ----- Invariants ----------------------------------------------------


def test_metadata_project_id_mismatch_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=r"metadata\.project_id must match"):
        ProjectContext(
            project_id=ProjectId("p-001"),
            metadata=_metadata("p-999"),
            patient_summary=_patient_summary(),
        )


def test_workflow_id_without_config_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="workflow_id and workflow_config"):
        _context(workflow_id="orthognathic", workflow_config=None)


def test_workflow_config_without_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="workflow_id and workflow_config"):
        _context(workflow_id=None, workflow_config={"steps": []})


# ----- with_workflow ------------------------------------------------


def test_with_workflow_returns_new_context() -> None:
    original = _context()
    updated = original.with_workflow(
        workflow_id="orthognathic",
        workflow_config={"steps": ["a"]},
    )

    assert updated is not original
    assert updated.workflow_id == "orthognathic"
    assert updated.workflow_config == {"steps": ["a"]}
    assert original.workflow_id is None


def test_with_workflow_rejects_empty_id() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="workflow_id"):
        _context().with_workflow(workflow_id="", workflow_config={})


def test_with_workflow_copies_config_dict() -> None:
    original = _context()
    source = {"steps": ["a"]}
    updated = original.with_workflow("wf", source)

    assert updated.workflow_config == {"steps": ["a"]}
    assert updated.workflow_config is not source


# ----- with_object_references ---------------------------------------


def test_with_object_references_returns_new_context() -> None:
    original = _context()
    ref = ObjectReference(object_id="ct-001", kind="volume", display_name="CT")
    updated = original.with_object_references((ref,))

    assert updated is not original
    assert updated.object_references == (ref,)
    assert original.object_references == ()


def test_with_object_references_preserves_workflow() -> None:
    original = _context(
        workflow_id="orthognathic",
        workflow_config={"steps": ["a"]},
    )
    updated = original.with_object_references(())

    assert updated.workflow_id == "orthognathic"
    assert updated.workflow_config == {"steps": ["a"]}


# ----- has_workflow -------------------------------------------------


def test_has_workflow_false_when_none() -> None:
    assert _context().has_workflow() is False


def test_has_workflow_true_when_set() -> None:
    ctx = _context(workflow_id="wf", workflow_config={})
    assert ctx.has_workflow() is True
