FILE: tests/unit/ui/editors/test_viewport_3d_mesh.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for Viewport3DView.set_mesh (STL validation helper)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import struct
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

pytest.importorskip("vtk")
pytest.importorskip("vtkmodules.qt")

from cranioz.infrastructure.geometry.io.stl import read_stl
from cranioz.ui.editors.viewport_3d.view import Viewport3DView


def _write_minimal_stl(path: Path) -> None:
    """Write a single-triangle binary STL, for tests only."""
    header = b"\x00" * 80
    body = struct.pack("<I", 1)
    triangle = (
        struct.pack("<3f", 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)
        + struct.pack("<3f", 0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        + struct.pack("<3f", 1.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        + struct.pack("<3f", 0.0, 1.0, 0.0)
        + struct.pack("<H", 0)
    )
    path.write_bytes(header + body + triangle)


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_set_mesh_adds_actor(qtbot, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    stl_path = tmp_path / "triangle.stl"
    _write_minimal_stl(stl_path)
    mesh = read_stl(stl_path)

    view = Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d")
    qtbot.addWidget(view.content_widget())

    view.set_mesh(mesh)

    assert view.has_mesh is True
    assert view.mesh is mesh
    assert view.renderer is not None
    props = view.renderer.GetViewProps()
    assert props.GetNumberOfItems() >= 1


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_set_mesh_replaces_previous(qtbot, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    stl_a = tmp_path / "a.stl"
    stl_b = tmp_path / "b.stl"
    _write_minimal_stl(stl_a)
    _write_minimal_stl(stl_b)
    mesh_a = read_stl(stl_a)
    mesh_b = read_stl(stl_b)

    view = Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d")
    qtbot.addWidget(view.content_widget())

    view.set_mesh(mesh_a)
    view.set_mesh(mesh_b)

    assert view.mesh is mesh_b


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_set_mesh_none_raises(qtbot) -> None:
    view = Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d")
    qtbot.addWidget(view.content_widget())

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="non-None Mesh"):
        view.set_mesh(None)  # type: ignore[arg-type]


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_dispose_clears_mesh_state(qtbot, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    stl_path = tmp_path / "triangle.stl"
    _write_minimal_stl(stl_path)
    mesh = read_stl(stl_path)

    view = Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d")
    qtbot.addWidget(view.content_widget())
    view.set_mesh(mesh)
    assert view.has_mesh is True

    view.dispose()

    assert view.has_mesh is False
    assert view.mesh is None



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/editors/test_viewport_3d_view.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for Viewport3DView (volume rendering)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import numpy as np
import pytest

pytest.importorskip("vtk")
pytest.importorskip("vtkmodules.qt")

from cranioz.domain.imaging.image_metadata import (
    ImageMetadata,
    ImageModality,
)
from cranioz.domain.imaging.volume import IntensityScale, Volume
from cranioz.domain.imaging.volume_geometry import VolumeGeometry
from cranioz.domain.imaging.volume_provenance import VolumeProvenance
from cranioz.ui.editors.viewport_3d.presets import (
    get_preset,
)
from cranioz.ui.editors.viewport_3d.view import Viewport3DView


def _make_volume() -> Volume:
    """Build a tiny synthetic ``Volume`` (4x4x4, int16).

    The volume is intentionally non-HU (``IntensityScale.UNKNOWN``)
    so it exercises the ``cbct`` branch of ``Viewport3DView.set_volume()``.
    """
    data = np.zeros((4, 4, 4), dtype=np.int16)
    geometry = VolumeGeometry.identity(
        dimensions=(4, 4, 4),
        spacing=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0),
        origin=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
    )
    return Volume(
        data=data,
        geometry=geometry,
        metadata=ImageMetadata(),
        intensity_scale=IntensityScale.UNKNOWN,
        provenance=VolumeProvenance(
            import_timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC),
        ),
        volume_id="volume-test",
    )


def _make_hu_volume() -> Volume:
    """Build a tiny HU-calibrated synthetic Volume (4x4x4, int16).

    Values are chosen inside a plausible HU range (air = -1000,
    bone = 1500) so the ``DicomCalibrator`` classification would
    agree with ``IntensityScale.HU``. This exercises the "HU volume"
    branch of ``Viewport3DView.set_volume()``.
    """
    data = np.full((4, 4, 4), -1000, dtype=np.int16)
    data[1:3, 1:3, 1:3] = 1500
    geometry = VolumeGeometry.identity(
        dimensions=(4, 4, 4),
        spacing=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0),
        origin=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
    )
    return Volume(
        data=data,
        geometry=geometry,
        metadata=ImageMetadata(modality=ImageModality.CT),
        intensity_scale=IntensityScale.HU,
        provenance=VolumeProvenance(
            import_timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC),
        ),
        volume_id="volume-test-hu",
    )


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_viewport_3d_initial_preset_before_volume_is_bone(qtbot) -> None:
    """The default preset before any volume is 'bone'.

    This is the *initial* state only: after ``set_volume()``, the preset
    is selected from the volume's ``IntensityScale`` (HU -> bone,
    otherwise -> cbct).
    """
    view = Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d")
    qtbot.addWidget(view.content_widget())
    assert view.preset is get_preset("bone")


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_viewport_3d_rejects_unknown_preset(qtbot) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="unknown volume preset"):
        Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d", preset="unknown")


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_viewport_3d_content_widget_returns_container(qtbot) -> None:
    view = Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d")
    widget = view.content_widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)
    assert widget is not None
    # The VTK pipeline is lazy: it is created on the first set_volume(),
    # so renderer is None until a volume is attached.
    assert not view.has_volume
    assert view.renderer is None


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_viewport_3d_set_volume_marks_has_volume(qtbot) -> None:
    volume = _make_volume()
    view = Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d")
    qtbot.addWidget(view.content_widget())

    view.set_volume(volume)

    assert view.has_volume is True
    assert view.renderer is not None
    assert view.volume_actor is not None


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_viewport_3d_retains_volume_reference(qtbot) -> None:
    """The View must retain a strong reference to the Volume (§8.1)."""
    volume = _make_volume()
    view = Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d")
    qtbot.addWidget(view.content_widget())

    view.set_volume(volume)
    assert view._volume is volume  # noqa: SLF001

    view.dispose()
    assert view._volume is None  # noqa: SLF001


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_viewport_3d_set_preset_changes_state(qtbot) -> None:
    """A non-HU volume starts at 'cbct'; set_preset() overrides it."""
    volume = _make_volume()  # IntensityScale.UNKNOWN
    view = Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d")
    qtbot.addWidget(view.content_widget())
    view.set_volume(volume)

    # Non-HU volume -> cbct preset, by design.
    assert view.preset is get_preset("cbct")

    view.set_preset("soft_tissue")
    assert view.preset is get_preset("soft_tissue")
    assert view.window == get_preset("soft_tissue").default_window
    assert view.level == get_preset("soft_tissue").default_level


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_viewport_3d_uses_bone_preset_for_hu_volume(qtbot) -> None:
    """An HU-calibrated volume starts at 'bone'."""
    volume = _make_hu_volume()
    view = Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d")
    qtbot.addWidget(view.content_widget())
    view.set_volume(volume)

    assert view.preset is get_preset("bone")


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_viewport_3d_set_window_level_updates_state(qtbot) -> None:
    volume = _make_volume()
    view = Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d")
    qtbot.addWidget(view.content_widget())
    view.set_volume(volume)

    view.set_window_level(1000.0, 200.0)
    assert view.window == 1000.0
    assert view.level == 200.0


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_viewport_3d_set_window_level_rejects_non_positive(qtbot) -> None:
    volume = _make_volume()
    view = Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d")
    qtbot.addWidget(view.content_widget())
    view.set_volume(volume)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="window must be positive"):
        view.set_window_level(0.0, 200.0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="window must be positive"):
        view.set_window_level(-100.0, 200.0)


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_viewport_3d_dispose_clears_state(qtbot) -> None:
    volume = _make_volume()
    view = Viewport3DView(view_id="view.3d")
    qtbot.addWidget(view.content_widget())
    view.set_volume(volume)
    assert view.has_volume

    view.dispose()

    assert view.has_volume is False
    assert view.renderer is None
    assert view.volume_actor is None



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/editors/test_view_layout_renderer.py
======================================================================

"""Unit tests for ViewLayoutRenderer.

The renderer materializes a ViewLayoutSpec into nested QSplitters
with each View's content_widget() at the Leaves.

These tests use a FakeView (a View subclass) whose content_widget()
returns a QLabel. The tests verify the structure of the rendered
widget tree.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QLabel, QSplitter
from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.ui.editors.view import View
from cranioz.ui.editors.view_layout_renderer import (
    ViewLayoutRenderer,
    ViewLayoutRendererError,
)
from cranioz.ui.editors.view_layout_spec import (
    SplitDirection,
    ViewLayoutSpec,
)


class FakeView(View):
    """A minimal View whose content_widget is a QLabel."""

    def __init__(self, view_id: str, view_type: str = "fake") -> None:
        super().__init__(view_id=view_id, view_type=view_type)
        self._label: QLabel | None = None

    def content_widget(self) -> QLabel:
        if self._label is None:
            self._label = QLabel(self.view_id)
            self._label.setObjectName(f"fake_view.{self.view_id}")
        return self._label


# ----- Fixtures -----------------------------------------------------


@pytest.fixture
def renderer() -> ViewLayoutRenderer:
    return ViewLayoutRenderer()


@pytest.fixture
def views() -> dict[str, View]:
    return {
        "view.a": FakeView("view.a"),
        "view.b": FakeView("view.b"),
        "view.c": FakeView("view.c"),
        "view.d": FakeView("view.d"),
    }


# ----- Single Leaf --------------------------------------------------


def test_render_single_leaf_returns_content_widget(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    renderer: ViewLayoutRenderer,
    views: dict[str, View],
) -> None:
    spec = ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.a")
    root = renderer.render(spec, views)
    qtbot.addWidget(root)

    assert isinstance(root, QLabel)
    assert root.text() == "view.a"


def test_render_single_leaf_missing_view_raises(
    renderer: ViewLayoutRenderer,
    views: dict[str, View],
) -> None:
    spec = ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.unknown")
    with pytest.raises(ViewLayoutRendererError, match="unknown"):
        renderer.render(spec, views)


# ----- Horizontal Split --------------------------------------------


def test_render_horizontal_split_returns_qsplitter(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    renderer: ViewLayoutRenderer,
    views: dict[str, View],
) -> None:
    spec = ViewLayoutSpec.split(
        SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
        (ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.a"), ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.b")),
    )
    root = renderer.render(spec, views)
    qtbot.addWidget(root)

    assert isinstance(root, QSplitter)
    assert root.orientation() == Qt.Horizontal
    assert root.count() == 2


def test_render_horizontal_split_children_order(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    renderer: ViewLayoutRenderer,
    views: dict[str, View],
) -> None:
    spec = ViewLayoutSpec.split(
        SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
        (ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.a"), ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.b")),
    )
    root = renderer.render(spec, views)
    qtbot.addWidget(root)

    assert isinstance(root, QSplitter)
    child_0 = root.widget(0)
    child_1 = root.widget(1)
    assert isinstance(child_0, QLabel)
    assert isinstance(child_1, QLabel)
    assert child_0.text() == "view.a"
    assert child_1.text() == "view.b"


# ----- Vertical Split ----------------------------------------------


def test_render_vertical_split_returns_qsplitter(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    renderer: ViewLayoutRenderer,
    views: dict[str, View],
) -> None:
    spec = ViewLayoutSpec.split(
        SplitDirection.VERTICAL,
        (ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.a"), ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.b")),
    )
    root = renderer.render(spec, views)
    qtbot.addWidget(root)

    assert isinstance(root, QSplitter)
    assert root.orientation() == Qt.Vertical


# ----- Nested Split (2x2) ------------------------------------------


def test_render_2x2_nested_split(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    renderer: ViewLayoutRenderer,
    views: dict[str, View],
) -> None:
    spec = ViewLayoutSpec.split(
        SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
        (
            ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.a"),
            ViewLayoutSpec.split(
                SplitDirection.VERTICAL,
                (
                    ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.b"),
                    ViewLayoutSpec.split(
                        SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
                        (
                            ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.c"),
                            ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.d"),
                        ),
                    ),
                ),
            ),
        ),
    )
    root = renderer.render(spec, views)
    qtbot.addWidget(root)

    assert isinstance(root, QSplitter)
    assert root.orientation() == Qt.Horizontal
    assert root.count() == 2

    # Left child is a leaf.
    left = root.widget(0)
    assert isinstance(left, QLabel)
    assert left.text() == "view.a"

    # Right child is a vertical split.
    right = root.widget(1)
    assert isinstance(right, QSplitter)
    assert right.orientation() == Qt.Vertical
    assert right.count() == 2

    # Top of the vertical split is view.b.
    top = right.widget(0)
    assert isinstance(top, QLabel)
    assert top.text() == "view.b"

    # Bottom of the vertical split is a horizontal split.
    bottom = right.widget(1)
    assert isinstance(bottom, QSplitter)
    assert bottom.orientation() == Qt.Horizontal
    assert bottom.count() == 2


# ----- Cache reuse --------------------------------------------------


def test_render_same_spec_twice_reuses_splitter(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    renderer: ViewLayoutRenderer,
    views: dict[str, View],
) -> None:
    spec = ViewLayoutSpec.split(
        SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
        (ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.a"), ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.b")),
    )
    first = renderer.render(spec, views)
    second = renderer.render(spec, views)
    qtbot.addWidget(first)

    assert first is second


def test_clear_cache_discards_splitters(
    qtbot: QtBot,
    renderer: ViewLayoutRenderer,
    views: dict[str, View],
) -> None:
    spec = ViewLayoutSpec.split(
        SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
        (ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.a"), ViewLayoutSpec.leaf("view.b")),
    )
    renderer.render(spec, views)
    renderer.clear_cache()

    # After clear_cache, a new render creates a new splitter.
    second = renderer.render(spec, views)
    qtbot.addWidget(second)
    assert isinstance(second, QSplitter)

"""Tests for the volume rendering presets."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.editors.viewport_3d.presets import (
    BONE,
    CBCT,
    CT_ANGIO,
    PRESETS,
    SKIN,
    SOFT_TISSUE,
    get_preset,
)


def test_presets_dict_contains_expected_names() -> None:
    assert set(PRESETS) == {
        "bone",
        "soft_tissue",
        "skin",
        "ct_angio",
        "cbct",
    }


def test_get_preset_returns_known() -> None:
    assert get_preset("bone") is BONE
    assert get_preset("soft_tissue") is SOFT_TISSUE
    assert get_preset("skin") is SKIN
    assert get_preset("ct_angio") is CT_ANGIO
    assert get_preset("cbct") is CBCT


def test_get_preset_unknown_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="unknown volume preset"):
        get_preset("does_not_exist")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("preset", PRESETS.values(), ids=PRESETS.keys())
def test_preset_has_non_empty_transfer_functions(preset) -> None:
    assert preset.color_points, f"{preset.name} has no color points"
    assert preset.opacity_points, f"{preset.name} has no opacity points"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("preset", PRESETS.values(), ids=PRESETS.keys())
def test_preset_window_is_positive(preset) -> None:
    assert preset.default_window > 0

"""Tests for TestEditor."""

# mypy: ignore-errors
# This file exercises the Editor contract with Qt widgets.

from __future__ import annotations

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QLabel
from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.ui.editors.builtin.test_editor import DummyEditor
from cranioz.ui.editors.editor_context import EditorContext
from cranioz.ui.toolbars.specification import ToolbarOrientation

# ----- Metadata ----------------------------------------------------


def test_editor_metadata() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a", title="Alpha")
    assert editor.editor_id == "editor.a"
    assert editor.title == "Alpha"


def test_default_editor_id_and_title() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor()
    assert editor.editor_id == "editor.test"
    assert editor.title == "Test Editor"


# ----- Content widget ----------------------------------------------


def test_content_widget_is_qlabel(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    widget = editor.content_widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)
    assert isinstance(widget, QLabel)


def test_content_widget_shows_editor_id(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    widget = editor.content_widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)
    assert widget.text() == "editor.a"


def test_content_widget_is_cached(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    widget1 = editor.content_widget()
    widget2 = editor.content_widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(widget1)
    assert widget1 is widget2


def test_content_widget_has_object_name(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    widget = editor.content_widget()
    qtbot.addWidget(widget)
    assert widget.objectName() == "dummy_editor.content.editor.a"


# ----- Toolbar specs ------------------------------------------------


def test_toolbar_specs_returns_one_spec() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    specs = editor.toolbar_specs()
    assert len(specs) == 1


def test_toolbar_spec_has_expected_id() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    spec = editor.toolbar_specs()[0]
    assert spec.toolbar_id == "toolbar.editor.a"


def test_toolbar_spec_has_log_and_toggle_tools() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    spec = editor.toolbar_specs()[0]
    assert "tool.log" in spec.tool_ids
    assert "tool.toggle" in spec.tool_ids


def test_toolbar_spec_respects_orientation() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(
        editor_id="editor.a",
        toolbar_orientation=ToolbarOrientation.VERTICAL,
    )
    spec = editor.toolbar_specs()[0]
    assert spec.orientation is ToolbarOrientation.VERTICAL


# ----- Persistence -------------------------------------------------


def test_persistent_state_before_initialize_is_empty() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    assert editor.persistent_state() == {}


def test_persistent_state_after_initialize() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    ctx = EditorContext(editor_id="editor.a", extras={"zoom": 2.0})
    editor.initialize(ctx)
    state = editor.persistent_state()
    assert state["editor_id"] == "editor.a"
    assert state["extras"] == {"zoom": 2.0}


def test_restore_state_is_noop() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    editor.restore_state({"any": "thing"})


# ----- Lifecycle ---------------------------------------------------


def test_initialize_stores_context() -> None:
    editor = DummyEditor(editor_id="editor.a")
    ctx = EditorContext(editor_id="editor.a")
    editor.initialize(ctx)
    assert editor.persistent_state()["editor_id"] == "editor.a"

======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/editors/tomography/test_reload_volume.py
======================================================================

"""Unit test: TomographyEditor.reload_volume() re-applies a new Volume."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime

import numpy as np
import pytest

pytest.importorskip("pydicom")
pytest.importorskip("vtk")
pytest.importorskip("vtkmodules.qt")

from cranioz.application.scene.scene_query import InMemorySceneQuery
from cranioz.domain.imaging.image_metadata import ImageMetadata
from cranioz.domain.imaging.volume import IntensityScale, Volume
from cranioz.domain.imaging.volume_geometry import VolumeGeometry
from cranioz.domain.imaging.volume_provenance import VolumeProvenance
from cranioz.domain.scene.domain_object_store import DomainObjectStore
from cranioz.ui.editors.editor_context import EditorContext
from cranioz.ui.editors.tomography.editor import TomographyEditor


def _make_volume() -> Volume:
    """Build a tiny synthetic ``Volume`` (4x4x4, int16)."""
    data = np.zeros((4, 4, 4), dtype=np.int16)
    geometry = VolumeGeometry.identity(
        dimensions=(4, 4, 4),
        spacing=(1.0, 1.0, 1.0),
        origin=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
    )
    return Volume(
        data=data,
        geometry=geometry,
        metadata=ImageMetadata(),
        intensity_scale=IntensityScale.UNKNOWN,
        provenance=VolumeProvenance(
            import_timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC),
        ),
        volume_id="volume-test",
    )


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_reload_volume_picks_up_new_volume(qtbot) -> None:
    store = DomainObjectStore()
    scene_query = InMemorySceneQuery()

    editor = TomographyEditor()
    editor.initialize(
        EditorContext(
            editor_id="editor.tomography",
            scene_query=scene_query,
            domain_object_store=store,
        )
    )
    qtbot.addWidget(editor.content_widget())

    # Initially, no volume in the store → no View has a volume.
    for view in editor.views():
        if hasattr(view, "has_volume"):
            assert not view.has_volume

    # Add a Volume to the store and to the Scene.
    volume = _make_volume()
    store.add("volume-1", volume)
    scene_query._add("volume-1", object())

    # Reload → the Views must pick up the volume.
    editor.reload_volume()

    for view in editor.views():
        if hasattr(view, "has_volume"):
            assert view.has_volume, f"{view.view_id} has no volume"

"""Tests for HomePage."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.application.event_bus import EventBus
from cranioz.application.flows.definition import (
    FlowDefinition,
    FlowStep,
)
from cranioz.application.project.in_memory_project_repository import (
    InMemoryProjectRepository,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_access_policy import (
    AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_catalog import ProjectCatalog
from cranioz.application.project.project_manager import ProjectManager
from cranioz.domain.patient.value_objects.patient_id import PatientId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.ui.home.home_page import HomePage


def _make_page() -> tuple[HomePage, ProjectCatalog, ProjectManager]:
    repo = InMemoryProjectRepository()
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repo)
    manager = ProjectManager(
        repository=repo,
        event_bus=EventBus(),
        access_policy=AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy(),
    )
    page = HomePage(catalog=catalog)
    return page, catalog, manager


def _seed(manager: ProjectManager, project_id: str, name: str) -> None:
    manager.create_project(
        "test",
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
        project_name=name,
        patient_id=PatientId("pat-001"),
        patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
    )


def _make_flow(flow_id: str = "orthognathic") -> FlowDefinition:
    return FlowDefinition(
        flow_id=flow_id,
        name_key=f"flows.{flow_id}.name",
        version=1,
        steps=(
            FlowStep(
                step_id=f"{flow_id}_s0",
                name_key=f"flows.{flow_id}.steps.s0",
                module_id="patient",
            ),
        ),
    )


def _select_flow(page: HomePage, flow_id: str = "orthognathic") -> None:
    """Populate the FlowList with one flow and select it."""
    page._flow_list_widget().set_flows((_make_flow(flow_id),))
    page._flow_list_widget()._on_row_selected(flow_id)


# ----- Composition --------------------------------------------------


def test_has_expected_object_name(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    assert page.objectName() == "home_page"


def test_starts_with_empty_table(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    assert page._project_table_widget().row_count() == 0


def test_refresh_reflects_new_projects(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, manager = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    page.refresh()
    assert page._project_table_widget().row_count() == 1


# ----- Signal forwarding -------------------------------------------


def test_sidebar_hidden_by_default(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    """The CommunitySidebar starts hidden (extra resource)."""
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    assert page._sidebar_widget().isHidden() is True


def test_top_bar_toggle_sidebar_signal_is_handled(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    """The ☰ toggles the CommunitySidebar visibility."""
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    page.show()
    qtbot.waitExposed(page)

    assert page._sidebar_widget().isHidden() is True

    page._top_bar_widget()._menu_button.click()
    assert page._sidebar_widget().isHidden() is False

    page._top_bar_widget()._menu_button.click()
    assert page._sidebar_widget().isHidden() is True


def test_top_bar_help_signal_is_forwarded(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(page.help_requested, timeout=1000):
        page._top_bar_widget()._help_button.click()


def test_top_bar_settings_signal_is_forwarded(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(page.settings_requested, timeout=1000):
        page._top_bar_widget()._settings_button.click()


def test_sidebar_community_signal_is_forwarded(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(page.community_requested, timeout=1000):
        page._sidebar_widget().button_for("community").click()


def test_new_project_signal_is_forwarded(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(page.new_project_requested, timeout=1000):
        page._project_table_widget()._new_button.click()


def test_manage_projects_signal_is_forwarded(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(page.manage_projects_requested, timeout=1000):
        page._project_table_widget()._manage_button.click()


# ----- Start button behavior ---------------------------------------


def test_start_button_disabled_initially(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    assert page._start_button_widget().isEnabled() is False


def test_start_button_stays_disabled_with_only_project(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, manager = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    page.refresh()
    page._project_table_widget()._table.selectRow(0)
    assert page._start_button_widget().isEnabled() is False


def test_start_button_stays_disabled_with_only_flow(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    _select_flow(page)
    assert page._start_button_widget().isEnabled() is False


def test_start_button_enables_with_project_and_flow(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, manager = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    page.refresh()

    page._project_table_widget()._table.selectRow(0)
    _select_flow(page)

    assert page._start_button_widget().isEnabled() is True


def test_start_button_emits_open_requested(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, manager = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    page.refresh()

    page._project_table_widget()._table.selectRow(0)
    _select_flow(page)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(page.project_open_requested, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        page._start_button_widget().click()

    assert blocker.args[0] == ProjectId("p-001")
    assert blocker.args[1] == "orthognathic"


# ----- Architectural contract --------------------------------------


def test_home_page_has_no_project_manager_reference(
    qtbot: QtBot,
) -> None:
    """HomePage must not hold a ProjectManager; only the ProjectCatalog."""
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    assert not hasattr(page, "_manager")


# ----- Bottom bar composition --------------------------------------


def test_bottom_bar_is_present(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    assert page._bottom_bar_widget() is not None
    assert page._bottom_bar_widget().start_button() is page._start_button_widget()


def test_bottom_bar_is_ancestor_of_start_button(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    page, _, _ = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    assert page._bottom_bar_widget().isAncestorOf(page._start_button_widget())


# ----- Regression: deselection / search filter ---------------------


def test_start_button_disables_when_selection_cleared(
    qtbot: QtBot,
) -> None:
    """Clearing the project table selection must disable StartButton."""
    page, _, manager = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    page.refresh()

    page._project_table_widget()._table.selectRow(0)
    _select_flow(page)

    assert page._start_button_widget().isEnabled() is True

    page._project_table_widget()._table.clearSelection()

    assert page._start_button_widget().isEnabled() is False


def test_start_button_disables_when_search_filters_selected_project(
    qtbot: QtBot,
) -> None:
    """Filtering the selected project away must disable the StartButton."""
    page, _, manager = _make_page()
    qtbot.addWidget(page)
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    _seed(manager, "p-002", "Beta")
    page.refresh()

    page._project_table_widget()._table.selectRow(0)  # Alpha
    _select_flow(page)
    assert page._start_button_widget().isEnabled() is True

    page._project_table_widget()._search_edit.setText("beta")

    assert page._start_button_widget().isEnabled() is False

"""Tests for CommunitySidebar."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.ui.home.widgets.community_sidebar import CommunitySidebar


def test_has_five_buttons(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    sidebar = CommunitySidebar()
    qtbot.addWidget(sidebar)
    for key in ("community", "library", "news", "feedback", "contribute"):
        assert sidebar.button_for(key) is not None


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("key", "signal_name"),
    [
        ("community", "community_requested"),
        ("library", "library_requested"),
        ("news", "news_requested"),
        ("feedback", "feedback_requested"),
        ("contribute", "contribute_requested"),
    ],
)
def test_buttons_emit_signals(qtbot: QtBot, key: str, signal_name: str) -> None:
    sidebar = CommunitySidebar()
    qtbot.addWidget(sidebar)
    signal = getattr(sidebar, signal_name)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(signal, timeout=1000):
        sidebar.button_for(key).click()

"""Tests for FlowList."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.application.flows.definition import (
    FlowDefinition,
    FlowStep,
)
from cranioz.ui.home.widgets.flow_list import FlowList


def _flow(flow_id: str, steps: int = 2) -> FlowDefinition:
    return FlowDefinition(
        flow_id=flow_id,
        name_key=f"flows.{flow_id}.name",
        version=1,
        steps=tuple(
            FlowStep(
                step_id=f"{flow_id}_s{i}",
                name_key=f"flows.{flow_id}.steps.s{i}",
                module_id=f"m{i}",
            )
            for i in range(steps)
        ),
    )


def test_starts_empty(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    flow_list = FlowList()
    qtbot.addWidget(flow_list)
    assert flow_list.flow_count() == 0
    assert flow_list.selected_flow_id() is None


def test_manage_button_emits_signal(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    flow_list = FlowList()
    qtbot.addWidget(flow_list)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(flow_list.manage_flows_requested, timeout=1000):
        flow_list._manage_button.click()


def test_set_flows_populates(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    flow_list = FlowList()
    qtbot.addWidget(flow_list)
    flow_list.set_flows((_flow("a"), _flow("b")))
    assert flow_list.flow_count() == 2


def test_selecting_flow_emits_signal(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    flow_list = FlowList()
    qtbot.addWidget(flow_list)
    flow_list.set_flows((_flow("a"),))

    with qtbot.waitSignal(flow_list.flow_selected, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        flow_list._on_row_selected("a")

    assert blocker.args[0] == "a"
    assert flow_list.selected_flow_id() == "a"


def test_deselecting_flow_emits_none(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    flow_list = FlowList()
    qtbot.addWidget(flow_list)
    flow_list.set_flows((_flow("a"),))
    flow_list._on_row_selected("a")

    with qtbot.waitSignal(flow_list.flow_selected, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        flow_list._on_row_selected(None)

    assert blocker.args[0] is None
    assert flow_list.selected_flow_id() is None


def test_set_flows_clears_selection(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    flow_list = FlowList()
    qtbot.addWidget(flow_list)
    flow_list.set_flows((_flow("a"),))
    flow_list._on_row_selected("a")
    assert flow_list.selected_flow_id() == "a"

    flow_list.set_flows((_flow("b"),))
    assert flow_list.selected_flow_id() is None


def test_selecting_a_second_flow_deselects_the_first(
    qtbot: QtBot,
) -> None:
    """Only one flow is selected at a time."""
    flow_list = FlowList()
    qtbot.addWidget(flow_list)
    flow_list.set_flows((_flow("a"), _flow("b")))

    flow_list._on_row_selected("a")
    assert flow_list._rows["a"].is_checked() is True
    assert flow_list._rows["b"].is_checked() is False

    flow_list._on_row_selected("b")
    assert flow_list._rows["a"].is_checked() is False
    assert flow_list._rows["b"].is_checked() is True
    assert flow_list.selected_flow_id() == "b"


def test_set_flows_sorts_by_flow_id(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    """Rendered order is by flow_id, independent of input order."""
    flow_list = FlowList()
    qtbot.addWidget(flow_list)

    # Input order: c, a, b. Rendered order must be a, b, c.
    flow_list.set_flows((_flow("c"), _flow("a"), _flow("b")))

    assert list(flow_list._rows.keys()) == ["a", "b", "c"]

FILE: tests/unit/ui/home/widgets/test_flow_row.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for FlowRow."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from PySide6.QtCore import QPointF, Qt
from PySide6.QtGui import QMouseEvent
from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.application.flows.definition import (
    FlowDefinition,
    FlowStep,
)
from cranioz.ui.home.widgets.flow_row import FlowRow


def _flow(flow_id: str = "f1", steps: int = 3) -> FlowDefinition:
    return FlowDefinition(
        flow_id=flow_id,
        name_key=f"flows.{flow_id}.name",
        version=1,
        steps=tuple(
            FlowStep(
                step_id=f"s{i}",
                name_key=f"flows.{flow_id}.steps.s{i}",
                module_id=f"m{i}",
            )
            for i in range(steps)
        ),
    )


def _identity(key: str) -> str:
    return key


def _click(row: FlowRow) -> None:
    """Simulate a left-button click on the row."""
    event = QMouseEvent(
        QMouseEvent.Type.MouseButtonPress,
        QPointF(5, 5),
        QPointF(5, 5),
        Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton,
        Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton,
        Qt.KeyboardModifier.NoModifier,
    )
    row.mousePressEvent(event)


@pytest.fixture
def row(qtbot: QtBot) -> FlowRow:
    r = FlowRow(_flow(), translate=_identity)
    qtbot.addWidget(r)
    return r


def test_row_holds_flow_id(row: FlowRow) -> None:
    assert row.flow_id == "f1"


def test_row_is_not_checked_by_default(row: FlowRow) -> None:
    assert row.is_checked() is False


def test_set_checked_toggles(row: FlowRow) -> None:
    row.set_checked(True)
    assert row.is_checked() is True
    row.set_checked(False)
    assert row.is_checked() is False


def test_click_selects_emits_flow_id(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    row = FlowRow(_flow(), translate=_identity)
    qtbot.addWidget(row)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(row.selected, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        _click(row)

    assert blocker.args[0] == "f1"
    assert row.is_checked() is True


def test_click_again_deselects_emits_none(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    row = FlowRow(_flow(), translate=_identity)
    qtbot.addWidget(row)
    row.set_checked(True)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(row.selected, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        _click(row)

    assert blocker.args[0] is None
    assert row.is_checked() is False

    qtbot.addWidget(bar)

"""Tests for ProjectTable."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pytestqt.qtbot import QtBot

from cranioz.application.event_bus import EventBus
from cranioz.application.project.in_memory_project_repository import (
    InMemoryProjectRepository,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_access_policy import (
    AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy,
)
from cranioz.application.project.project_catalog import ProjectCatalog
from cranioz.application.project.project_manager import ProjectManager
from cranioz.domain.patient.value_objects.patient_id import PatientId
from cranioz.domain.project.project_id import ProjectId
from cranioz.ui.home.widgets.project_table import ProjectTable


def _make_catalog_and_manager() -> tuple[ProjectCatalog, ProjectManager]:
    repo = InMemoryProjectRepository()
    catalog = ProjectCatalog(repository=repo)
    manager = ProjectManager(
        repository=repo,
        event_bus=EventBus(),
        access_policy=AllowAllProjectAccessPolicy(),
    )
    return catalog, manager


def _seed(manager: ProjectManager, project_id: str, name: str) -> None:
    manager.create_project(
        "test",
        project_id=ProjectId(project_id),
        project_name=name,
        patient_id=PatientId("pat-001"),
        patient_display_name="Gabriel Silva",
        clinical_procedure="orthognathic",
    )


def test_starts_empty(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    catalog, _ = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)
    assert table.row_count() == 0


def test_lists_projects(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    catalog, manager = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    _seed(manager, "p-002", "Beta")
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)
    assert table.row_count() == 2


def test_search_filters(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    catalog, manager = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    _seed(manager, "p-002", "Beta")
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)

    table._search_edit.setText("alpha")
    assert table.row_count() == 1


def test_search_empty_shows_all(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    catalog, manager = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    _seed(manager, "p-002", "Beta")
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)

    table._search_edit.setText("")
    assert table.row_count() == 2


def test_selection_emits_signal(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    catalog, manager = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(table.project_selected, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        table._table.selectRow(0)

    assert blocker.args[0] == ProjectId("p-001")


def test_manage_button_emits_signal(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    """The 'Gerir projetos' button emits manage_projects_requested (no arg)."""
    catalog, _ = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(table.manage_projects_requested, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        table._manage_button.click()

    # No argument: managing projects is not about a specific project.
    assert blocker.args == []


def test_manage_button_is_always_enabled(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    """The 'Gerir projetos' button does not depend on selection."""
    catalog, manager = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)
    assert table._manage_button.isEnabled() is True


def test_new_button_emits_signal(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    catalog, _ = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(table.new_project_requested, timeout=1000):
        table._new_button.click()


def test_refresh_reloads(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    catalog, manager = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)
    assert table.row_count() == 0

    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    table.refresh()
    assert table.row_count() == 1


# ----- Selection lifecycle (None emission) ---------------------------


def test_clearing_selection_emits_none(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    """Deselect the only row: ProjectTable must emit project_selected(None).

    Regression guard: previously _on_selection_changed only emitted
    when a row was selected, so HomePage kept a stale _selected_project_id
    and the StartButton stayed enabled after the row was deselected.
    """
    catalog, manager = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)

    table._table.selectRow(0)
    assert table.selected_project_id() == ProjectId("p-001")

    with qtbot.waitSignal(table.project_selected, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        table._table.clearSelection()

    assert blocker.args[0] is None
    assert table.selected_project_id() is None


def test_refresh_preserves_selection_when_project_still_present(
    qtbot: QtBot,
) -> None:
    """refresh() must NOT clear the selection if the same project remains."""
    catalog, manager = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    _seed(manager, "p-002", "Beta")
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)

    table._table.selectRow(0)
    assert table.selected_project_id() == ProjectId("p-001")

    table.refresh()

    assert table.selected_project_id() == ProjectId("p-001")


def test_refresh_preserves_selection_through_unchanged_search(
    qtbot: QtBot,
) -> None:
    """Selecting a row, then refreshing, emits no None to the HomePage."""
    catalog, manager = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)

    table._table.selectRow(0)

    emitted: list[object] = []
    table.project_selected.connect(emitted.append)
    table.refresh()

    # No None emitted, because the project is still in the results.
    assert None not in emitted


def test_refresh_emits_none_when_selected_project_is_filtered_out(
    qtbot: QtBot,
) -> None:
    """Search filtering the selected project away must clear HomePage state."""
    catalog, manager = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    _seed(manager, "p-002", "Beta")
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)

    table._table.selectRow(0)  # Alpha, p-001
    assert table.selected_project_id() == ProjectId("p-001")

    with qtbot.waitSignal(table.project_selected, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        table._search_edit.setText("beta")  # filters out Alpha

    assert blocker.args[0] is None
    assert table.selected_project_id() is None
    assert table.row_count() == 1


def test_populate_does_not_emit_none_when_nothing_was_selected(
    qtbot: QtBot,
) -> None:
    """An initial or repeated refresh with no prior selection must stay silent."""
    catalog, manager = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)

    emitted: list[object] = []
    table.project_selected.connect(emitted.append)
    table.refresh()

    assert emitted == []


def test_populate_blocks_intermediate_signals(qtbot: QtBot) -> None:
    """During repopulation, only the final selection state is emitted.

    If blockSignals() were missing, clearSelection() inside _populate
    would emit None, then selectRow() would emit the ProjectId — the
    HomePage would see a None-then-id flicker on every refresh.
    """
    catalog, manager = _make_catalog_and_manager()
    _seed(manager, "p-001", "Alpha")
    _seed(manager, "p-002", "Beta")
    table = ProjectTable(catalog)
    qtbot.addWidget(table)

    table._table.selectRow(0)  # p-001

    emitted: list[object] = []
    table.project_selected.connect(emitted.append)
    table.refresh()

    # Exactly one emission (if any), and it must be the ProjectId,
    # never a bare None.
    assert None not in emitted

FILE: tests/unit/ui/theme/test_compiler.py

"""Tests for ThemeCompiler and CompiledStyles."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication

from cranioz.ui.theme import compiler as compiler_module
from cranioz.ui.theme.compiler import (
    CompiledStyles,
    ThemeCompiler,
    _compose_rgba,
    _hex_to_rgb,
)
from cranioz.ui.theme.tokens import (
    DARK_TOKENS,
    LIGHT_TOKENS,
    ElevationSpec,
    ThemeName,
)

# ----- _hex_to_rgb ---------------------------------------------------


class _FakeQApplication(QApplication):
    """QApplication subclass whose instance() always returns None.

    Only the static method is consulted by ThemeCompiler.apply(); the
    class is never instantiated.
    """

    @staticmethod
    def instance() -> None:
        return None


def test_hex_to_rgb_black() -> None:
    assert _hex_to_rgb("#000000") == (0, 0, 0)


def test_hex_to_rgb_white() -> None:
    assert _hex_to_rgb("#FFFFFF") == (255, 255, 255)


def test_hex_to_rgb_mixed() -> None:
    assert _hex_to_rgb("#1E1E1E") == (30, 30, 30)


def test_hex_to_rgb_rejects_short_hex() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        _hex_to_rgb("#FFF")


def test_hex_to_rgb_rejects_non_hash() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        _hex_to_rgb("1E1E1E")


# ----- _compose_rgba -------------------------------------------------


def test_compose_rgba_full_opacity() -> None:
    assert _compose_rgba("#1E1E1E", 1.0) == "rgba(30,30,30,1.0000)"


def test_compose_rgba_half_opacity() -> None:
    assert _compose_rgba("#252526", 0.92) == "rgba(37,37,38,0.9200)"


def test_compose_rgba_zero_opacity() -> None:
    assert _compose_rgba("#000000", 0.0) == "rgba(0,0,0,0.0000)"


# ----- compile: happy path -------------------------------------------


def test_compile_dark_returns_compiled_styles() -> None:
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    styles = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    assert isinstance(styles, CompiledStyles)
    assert styles.theme_name is ThemeName.DARK
    assert styles.qss  # non-empty
    assert "toolbar" in styles.surface_colors
    assert "popover" in styles.surface_colors
    assert "modal_backdrop" in styles.surface_colors


def test_compile_light_returns_compiled_styles() -> None:
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    styles = compiler.compile(LIGHT_TOKENS)
    assert styles.theme_name is ThemeName.LIGHT
    assert styles.qss


def test_compile_is_deterministic() -> None:
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    a = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    b = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    assert a.qss == b.qss
    assert a.surface_colors == b.surface_colors
    assert a.elevations == b.elevations


# ----- surface composition -------------------------------------------


def test_toolbar_surface_uses_background_panel_dark() -> None:
    """Dark toolbar = #242426 at 0.92."""
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    styles = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    assert styles.surface_color("toolbar") == "rgba(36,36,38,0.9200)"


def test_toolbar_surface_uses_background_panel_light() -> None:
    """Light toolbar = #FFFFFF at 0.92."""
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    styles = compiler.compile(LIGHT_TOKENS)
    assert styles.surface_color("toolbar") == "rgba(255,255,255,0.9200)"


def test_popover_surface_uses_background_elevated_dark() -> None:
    """Dark popover = #2C2C2E at 0.96."""
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    styles = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    assert styles.surface_color("popover") == "rgba(44,44,46,0.9600)"


def test_modal_backdrop_uses_different_opacity_per_theme() -> None:
    """Dark = #000000 at 0.60, Light = #000000 at 0.40."""
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    dark = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    light = compiler.compile(LIGHT_TOKENS)
    assert dark.surface_color("modal_backdrop") == "rgba(0,0,0,0.6000)"
    assert light.surface_color("modal_backdrop") == "rgba(0,0,0,0.4000)"


def test_surface_color_unknown_role_raises() -> None:
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    styles = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="unknown surface role"):
        styles.surface_color("not-a-role")


# ----- elevations ----------------------------------------------------


def test_elevations_are_exposed() -> None:
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    styles = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    assert isinstance(styles.elevation("panel"), ElevationSpec)
    assert isinstance(styles.elevation("dialog"), ElevationSpec)
    assert isinstance(styles.elevation("overlay"), ElevationSpec)


def test_elevation_panel_values_dark() -> None:
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    styles = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    panel = styles.elevation("panel")
    assert panel.offset_x == 0
    assert panel.offset_y == 1
    assert panel.blur_radius == 3
    assert panel.color == "rgba(0,0,0,0.40)"


def test_elevation_unknown_role_raises() -> None:
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    styles = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="unknown elevation role"):
        styles.elevation("not-a-role")


# ----- QSS sanity ----------------------------------------------------


def test_qss_contains_background_color() -> None:
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    styles = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    assert DARK_TOKENS.colors.background_application in styles.qss


def test_qss_contains_border_radius() -> None:
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    styles = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    assert f"border-radius: {DARK_TOKENS.borders.radius_control}px" in styles.qss


def test_qss_differs_between_themes() -> None:
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    dark_qss = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS).qss
    light_qss = compiler.compile(LIGHT_TOKENS).qss
    assert dark_qss != light_qss


# ----- apply: with QApplication (uses qapp fixture) ------------------


def test_apply_sets_application_stylesheet(qapp: QApplication) -> None:
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    styles = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)

    # Reset any stylesheet from other tests.
    qapp.setStyleSheet("")
    assert qapp.styleSheet() == ""

    compiler.apply(styles)

    assert qapp.styleSheet() == styles.qss


def test_apply_replaces_previous_stylesheet(qapp: QApplication) -> None:
    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    dark = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    light = compiler.compile(LIGHT_TOKENS)

    compiler.apply(dark)
    assert qapp.styleSheet() == dark.qss

    compiler.apply(light)
    assert qapp.styleSheet() == light.qss


# ----- apply: without QApplication ----------------------------------


def test_apply_without_qapplication_logs_warning(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture,
) -> None:
    """When no QApplication exists, apply() logs a warning and returns."""
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        compiler_module,
        "QApplication",
        _FakeQApplication,
    )

    compiler = ThemeCompiler()
    styles = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)

    with caplog.at_level("WARNING", logger="cranioz.ui.theme.compiler"):
        compiler.apply(styles)

    assert "skipping global stylesheet application" in caplog.text

FILE: tests/unit/ui/theme/test_service.py

"""Tests for ThemeService."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from PySide6.QtCore import QObject

from cranioz.infrastructure.settings.in_memory_settings_service import (
    InMemorySettingsService,
)
from cranioz.infrastructure.settings.keys import APPEARANCE_THEME_MODE
from cranioz.ui.theme import service as service_module
from cranioz.ui.theme.compiler import CompiledStyles
from cranioz.ui.theme.service import ThemeService
from cranioz.ui.theme.tokens import (
    DARK_TOKENS,
    LIGHT_TOKENS,
    ThemeMode,
    ThemeName,
    ThemeTokens,
)

# ----- Helpers -------------------------------------------------------


def _make(
    *,
    mode: ThemeMode = ThemeMode.DARK,
    settings: InMemorySettingsService | None = None,
) -> ThemeService:
    return ThemeService(
        settings=settings or InMemorySettingsService(),
        default_mode=mode,
    )


# ----- Construction and mode ------------------------------------------


def test_default_mode_is_dark() -> None:
    service = _make()
    assert service.mode() is ThemeMode.DARK
    assert service.theme() is ThemeName.DARK


def test_default_mode_can_be_light() -> None:
    service = _make(mode=ThemeMode.LIGHT)
    assert service.mode() is ThemeMode.LIGHT
    assert service.theme() is ThemeName.LIGHT


def test_persisted_mode_overrides_default() -> None:
    settings = InMemorySettingsService()
    settings.seed(APPEARANCE_THEME_MODE, ThemeMode.LIGHT.value)

    service = ThemeService(settings=settings, default_mode=ThemeMode.DARK)

    assert service.mode() is ThemeMode.LIGHT
    assert service.theme() is ThemeName.LIGHT


def test_invalid_persisted_mode_falls_back_to_default(
    caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture,
) -> None:
    settings = InMemorySettingsService()
    settings.seed(APPEARANCE_THEME_MODE, "not-a-theme")

    with caplog.at_level("WARNING", logger="cranioz.ui.theme.service"):
        service = ThemeService(
            settings=settings,
            default_mode=ThemeMode.DARK,
        )

    assert service.mode() is ThemeMode.DARK
    assert service.theme() is ThemeName.DARK
    assert "invalid appearance.theme_mode" in caplog.text


# ----- set_mode -------------------------------------------------------


def test_set_mode_updates_mode_and_theme() -> None:
    service = _make()
    service.set_mode(ThemeMode.LIGHT)
    assert service.mode() is ThemeMode.LIGHT
    assert service.theme() is ThemeName.LIGHT


def test_set_mode_same_mode_is_noop() -> None:
    service = _make()
    emitted: list[ThemeName] = []
    service.theme_changed.connect(emitted.append)

    service.set_mode(ThemeMode.DARK)

    assert emitted == []


def test_set_mode_emits_theme_changed() -> None:
    service = _make()
    emitted: list[ThemeName] = []
    service.theme_changed.connect(emitted.append)

    service.set_mode(ThemeMode.LIGHT)

    assert emitted == [ThemeName.LIGHT]


def test_set_mode_does_not_persist() -> None:
    settings = InMemorySettingsService()
    service = ThemeService(settings=settings, default_mode=ThemeMode.DARK)

    changes: list[str] = []
    settings.changed.connect(changes.append)

    service.set_mode(ThemeMode.LIGHT)

    # set_mode must not call settings.set(); persistence is the caller's job.
    assert changes == []
    assert settings.get(APPEARANCE_THEME_MODE, ThemeMode.DARK.value) == ThemeMode.DARK.value


# ----- tokens and accessors -------------------------------------------


def test_tokens_returns_dark_tokens() -> None:
    service = _make()
    assert service.tokens() is DARK_TOKENS


def test_tokens_returns_light_tokens() -> None:
    service = _make(mode=ThemeMode.LIGHT)
    assert service.tokens() is LIGHT_TOKENS


def test_color_returns_expected_value() -> None:
    service = _make()
    assert service.color("background_application") == "#1C1C1E"
    assert service.color("text_primary") == "#E5E5E7"


def test_color_unknown_path_raises() -> None:
    service = _make()
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="unknown color token"):
        service.color("does_not_exist")


def test_dimension_returns_expected_value() -> None:
    service = _make()
    assert service.dimension("control_height") == 28
    assert service.dimension("icon_size") == 20


def test_dimension_unknown_path_raises() -> None:
    service = _make()
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="unknown dimension token"):
        service.dimension("does_not_exist")


def test_surface_color_toolbar_is_composed() -> None:
    service = _make()
    # Dark toolbar = background_panel #242426 at 0.92 opacity.
    assert service.surface_color("toolbar") == "rgba(36,36,38,0.9200)"


def test_surface_color_unknown_role_raises() -> None:
    service = _make()
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="unknown surface role"):
        service.surface_color("does_not_exist")


# ----- compiled and compiler ------------------------------------------


def test_compiled_returns_compiled_styles() -> None:
    service = _make()
    compiled = service.compiled()
    assert isinstance(compiled, CompiledStyles)
    assert compiled.theme_name is ThemeName.DARK


def test_compiler_accessor() -> None:
    service = _make()
    compiler = service.compiler()
    # Compiling the same tokens twice must produce equivalent surfaces.
    a = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    b = compiler.compile(DARK_TOKENS)
    assert a.surface_colors == b.surface_colors
    assert a.qss == b.qss


# ----- icon_name (legacy, kept for Sprint F) --------------------------


def test_icon_name_is_identity() -> None:
    service = _make()
    assert service.icon_name("log") == "log"
    assert service.icon_name("toggle") == "toggle"


def test_icon_name_is_theme_agnostic() -> None:
    dark = _make(mode=ThemeMode.DARK)
    light = _make(mode=ThemeMode.LIGHT)
    assert dark.icon_name("x") == light.icon_name("x")


def test_empty_icon_name_raises() -> None:
    service = _make()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="logical_name"):
        service.icon_name("")


# ----- theme_changed signal and system resolution --------------------


def test_system_mode_falls_back_to_dark_without_qapplication() -> None:
    """In tests without a running QGuiApplication, SYSTEM resolves to DARK."""
    service = _make(mode=ThemeMode.SYSTEM)
    assert service.theme() is ThemeName.DARK


def test_system_mode_reacts_to_color_scheme_change(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    """When SYSTEM is active and colorSchemeChanged fires, the theme updates."""

    forced_theme = ThemeName.LIGHT
    monkeypatch.setattr(service_module, "_resolve_system_theme", lambda: forced_theme)

    service = _make(mode=ThemeMode.SYSTEM)

    # Simulate the OS preference change by invoking the private slot.
    service._on_color_scheme_changed(None)  # noqa: SLF001

    assert service.theme() is ThemeName.LIGHT


def test_non_system_mode_ignores_color_scheme_change(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:

    monkeypatch.setattr(service_module, "_resolve_system_theme", lambda: ThemeName.LIGHT)

    service = _make(mode=ThemeMode.DARK)

    service._on_color_scheme_changed(None)  # noqa: SLF001

    assert service.theme() is ThemeName.DARK


# ----- invariants -----------------------------------------------------


def test_theme_service_is_a_qobject() -> None:
    service = _make()
    assert isinstance(service, QObject)


def test_tokens_type_matches_theme() -> None:
    service = _make(mode=ThemeMode.LIGHT)
    tokens: ThemeTokens = service.tokens()
    assert tokens.name is ThemeName.LIGHT


def test_theme_changed_signal_exists() -> None:
    service = _make()
    # Sanity: the signal is declared and connectable.
    service.theme_changed.connect(lambda _name: None)

FILE: tests/unit/ui/theme/test_tokens.py

"""Tests for the canonical design tokens."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
from collections.abc import Callable

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.theme.tokens import (
    DARK_TOKENS,
    LIGHT_TOKENS,
    THEME_TOKENS,
    BorderTokens,
    ColorTokens,
    DimensionTokens,
    EffectTokens,
    ElevationSpec,
    SpacingTokens,
    SurfaceTokens,
    ThemeMode,
    ThemeName,
    ThemeTokens,
    TypographyTokens,
    count_tokens,
    validate_all_themes,
    validate_tokens,
)

# ----- Enums ---------------------------------------------------------


def test_theme_mode_is_str_enum() -> None:
    assert issubclass(ThemeMode, str)
    assert ThemeMode.DARK.value == "dark"
    assert ThemeMode.LIGHT.value == "light"
    assert ThemeMode.SYSTEM.value == "system"


def test_theme_name_is_str_enum() -> None:
    assert issubclass(ThemeName, str)
    assert ThemeName.DARK.value == "dark"
    assert ThemeName.LIGHT.value == "light"


def test_theme_name_has_no_system_member() -> None:
    assert "SYSTEM" not in ThemeName.__members__


def test_theme_mode_members() -> None:
    assert {m.name for m in ThemeMode} == {"DARK", "LIGHT", "SYSTEM"}


def test_theme_name_members() -> None:
    assert {m.name for m in ThemeName} == {"DARK", "LIGHT"}


# ----- Canonical values ---------------------------------------------


def test_count_tokens_is_47() -> None:
    """Phase 2 closes at 47 tokens across 7 categories."""
    assert count_tokens() == 47


def test_theme_tokens_cover_all_concrete_names() -> None:
    assert set(THEME_TOKENS) == {ThemeName.DARK, ThemeName.LIGHT}


def test_dark_tokens_name_matches_key() -> None:
    assert DARK_TOKENS.name is ThemeName.DARK
    assert THEME_TOKENS[ThemeName.DARK] is DARK_TOKENS


def test_light_tokens_name_matches_key() -> None:
    assert LIGHT_TOKENS.name is ThemeName.LIGHT
    assert THEME_TOKENS[ThemeName.LIGHT] is LIGHT_TOKENS


# ----- Completeness across themes -----------------------------------


def _field_names(dc_type: type) -> set[str]:
    return {f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(dc_type)}


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "dc_type",
    [
        ColorTokens,
        TypographyTokens,
        SpacingTokens,
        DimensionTokens,
        BorderTokens,
        EffectTokens,
        SurfaceTokens,
    ],
)
def test_dark_and_light_have_identical_structure(dc_type: type) -> None:
    dark_field_names = _field_names(dc_type)
    dark_value = getattr(DARK_TOKENS, _category_for(dc_type))
    light_value = getattr(LIGHT_TOKENS, _category_for(dc_type))
    assert isinstance(dark_value, dc_type)
    assert isinstance(light_value, dc_type)
    for name in dark_field_names:
        assert hasattr(dark_value, name)
        assert hasattr(light_value, name)


def _category_for(dc_type: type) -> str:
    return {
        ColorTokens: "colors",
        TypographyTokens: "typography",
        SpacingTokens: "spacing",
        DimensionTokens: "dimensions",
        BorderTokens: "borders",
        EffectTokens: "effects",
        SurfaceTokens: "surfaces",
    }[dc_type]


# ----- Validation: happy path ---------------------------------------


def test_validate_all_themes_passes() -> None:
    validate_all_themes()


def test_validate_tokens_accepts_dark() -> None:
    validate_tokens(DARK_TOKENS)


def test_validate_tokens_accepts_light() -> None:
    validate_tokens(LIGHT_TOKENS)


# ----- Validation: failure modes ------------------------------------


def _mutated(tokens: ThemeTokens, **color_overrides: str) -> ThemeTokens:
    new_colors = dataclasses.replace(tokens.colors, **color_overrides)
    return dataclasses.replace(tokens, colors=new_colors)


def _assert_raises_with_message(
    fn: Callable[[], None],
    expected_substring: str,
) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError) as exc_info:
        fn()
    assert expected_substring in str(exc_info.value)


def test_invalid_hex_color_rejected() -> None:
    bad = _mutated(DARK_TOKENS, background_application="#XYZ")
    _assert_raises_with_message(lambda: validate_tokens(bad), "color.background_application")


def test_short_hex_color_rejected() -> None:
    bad = _mutated(DARK_TOKENS, background_application="#FFF")
    _assert_raises_with_message(lambda: validate_tokens(bad), "color.background_application")


def test_invalid_rgba_color_rejected() -> None:
    bad = _mutated(DARK_TOKENS, text_primary="rgba(300,0,0,0.5)")
    _assert_raises_with_message(lambda: validate_tokens(bad), "color.text_primary")


def test_effect_invalid_color_rejected() -> None:
    bad_effects = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS.effects, hover_overlay="not-a-color")
    bad = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS, effects=bad_effects)
    _assert_raises_with_message(lambda: validate_tokens(bad), "effect.hover_overlay")


def test_effect_negative_blur_rejected() -> None:
    bad_spec = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS.effects.elevation_panel, blur_radius=-1)
    bad_effects = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS.effects, elevation_panel=bad_spec)
    bad = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS, effects=bad_effects)
    _assert_raises_with_message(lambda: validate_tokens(bad), "blur_radius")


def test_surface_opacity_above_one_rejected() -> None:
    bad_surfaces = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS.surfaces, opacity_toolbar=1.01)
    bad = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS, surfaces=bad_surfaces)
    _assert_raises_with_message(lambda: validate_tokens(bad), "surface.opacity_toolbar")


def test_surface_opacity_below_zero_rejected() -> None:
    bad_surfaces = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS.surfaces, opacity_modal_backdrop=-0.1)
    bad = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS, surfaces=bad_surfaces)
    _assert_raises_with_message(lambda: validate_tokens(bad), "surface.opacity_modal_backdrop")


def test_negative_spacing_rejected() -> None:
    bad_spacing = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS.spacing, md=-1)
    bad = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS, spacing=bad_spacing)
    _assert_raises_with_message(lambda: validate_tokens(bad), "spacing.md")


def test_negative_dimension_rejected() -> None:
    bad_dimensions = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS.dimensions, control_height=-1)
    bad = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS, dimensions=bad_dimensions)
    _assert_raises_with_message(lambda: validate_tokens(bad), "dimension.control_height")


def test_negative_border_rejected() -> None:
    bad_borders = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS.borders, radius_control=-1)
    bad = dataclasses.replace(DARK_TOKENS, borders=bad_borders)
    _assert_raises_with_message(lambda: validate_tokens(bad), "border.radius_control")


# ----- Semantic values: a few spot checks ---------------------------


def test_dark_and_light_have_different_backgrounds() -> None:
    assert DARK_TOKENS.colors.background_application != (LIGHT_TOKENS.colors.background_application)


def test_modal_backdrop_opacity_differs_by_theme() -> None:
    """Per the spec, modal backdrop opacity is 0.60 in DARK, 0.40 in LIGHT."""
    assert DARK_TOKENS.surfaces.opacity_modal_backdrop == 0.60
    assert LIGHT_TOKENS.surfaces.opacity_modal_backdrop == 0.40


def test_toolbar_and_popover_opacity_are_theme_invariant() -> None:
    assert DARK_TOKENS.surfaces.opacity_toolbar == (LIGHT_TOKENS.surfaces.opacity_toolbar)
    assert DARK_TOKENS.surfaces.opacity_popover == (LIGHT_TOKENS.surfaces.opacity_popover)


def test_typography_sizes_match_spec() -> None:
    for tokens in (DARK_TOKENS, LIGHT_TOKENS):
        assert tokens.typography.size_caption == 11
        assert tokens.typography.size_body == 13
        assert tokens.typography.size_label == 14
        assert tokens.typography.size_heading == 18


def test_spacing_scale_matches_spec() -> None:
    for tokens in (DARK_TOKENS, LIGHT_TOKENS):
        assert tokens.spacing.xs == 4
        assert tokens.spacing.sm == 8
        assert tokens.spacing.md == 16
        assert tokens.spacing.lg == 24
        assert tokens.spacing.xl == 32


def test_elevation_specs_are_typed() -> None:
    for tokens in (DARK_TOKENS, LIGHT_TOKENS):
        assert isinstance(tokens.effects.elevation_panel, ElevationSpec)
        assert isinstance(tokens.effects.elevation_dialog, ElevationSpec)
        assert isinstance(tokens.effects.elevation_overlay, ElevationSpec)


# ----- Immutability --------------------------------------------------


def test_theme_tokens_are_frozen() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(DARK_TOKENS, "name", ThemeName.LIGHT)  # noqa: B010


def test_color_tokens_are_frozen() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        setattr(DARK_TOKENS.colors, "text_primary", "#000000")  # noqa: B010

FILE: tests/unit/ui/toolbars/test_item.py

"""Tests for ToolbarItem types."""

# mypy: ignore-errors
# This file intentionally mutates frozen dataclasses to test immutability.

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.toolbars.item import (
    ActionItem,
    Separator,
    ToggleItem,
    ToolbarItemKind,
)

# ----- ActionItem --------------------------------------------------


def test_action_item_holds_fields() -> None:
    item = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="Log")
    assert item.tool_id == "tool.log"
    assert item.label == "Log"
    assert item.icon is None
    assert item.kind is ToolbarItemKind.ACTION


def test_action_item_with_icon() -> None:
    item = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="Log", icon="log")
    assert item.icon == "log"


def test_action_item_requires_tool_id() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="tool_id"):
        ActionItem(tool_id="", label="Log")


def test_action_item_requires_label() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="label"):
        ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="")


# ----- ToggleItem --------------------------------------------------


def test_toggle_item_defaults() -> None:
    item = ToggleItem(tool_id="tool.grid", label="Grid")
    assert item.tool_id == "tool.grid"
    assert item.label == "Grid"
    assert item.icon is None
    assert item.checked is False
    assert item.kind is ToolbarItemKind.TOGGLE


def test_toggle_item_checked_true() -> None:
    item = ToggleItem(tool_id="tool.grid", label="Grid", checked=True)
    assert item.checked is True


def test_toggle_item_requires_tool_id() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="tool_id"):
        ToggleItem(tool_id="", label="Grid")


def test_toggle_item_requires_label() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="label"):
        ToggleItem(tool_id="tool.grid", label="")


# ----- Separator ---------------------------------------------------


def test_separator_has_separator_kind() -> None:
    sep = Separator()
    assert sep.kind is ToolbarItemKind.SEPARATOR


def test_separators_are_equal() -> None:
    assert Separator() == Separator()


# ----- Equality and immutability -----------------------------------


def test_action_items_with_same_data_are_equal() -> None:
    a = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="Log")
    b = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="Log")
    assert a == b


def test_action_items_with_different_data_are_not_equal() -> None:
    a = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="Log")
    b = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.other", label="Other")
    assert a != b


def test_action_item_is_frozen() -> None:
    item = ActionItem(tool_id="tool.log", label="Log")
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        item.label = "Other"


def test_toggle_item_is_frozen() -> None:
    item = ToggleItem(tool_id="tool.grid", label="Grid")
    with pytest.raises(dataclasses.FrozenInstanceError):
        item.checked = True


# ----- Enum --------------------------------------------------------


def test_toolbar_item_kind_members() -> None:
    expected = {"ACTION", "TOGGLE", "SEPARATOR"}
    actual = {member.name for member in ToolbarItemKind}
    assert actual == expected

"""Tests for ToolbarState."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.toolbars.specification import ToolbarVisibilityMode
from cranioz.ui.toolbars.state import ToolbarState, ToolbarStateError


def test_toolbar_state_defaults() -> None:
    state = ToolbarState(toolbar_id="tb.x")
    assert state.toolbar_id == "tb.x"
    assert state.visible is True


def test_initial_for_always_is_visible() -> None:
    state = ToolbarState.initial_for("tb.x", ToolbarVisibilityMode.ALWAYS)
    assert state.visible is True


def test_initial_for_collapsible_is_visible() -> None:
    state = ToolbarState.initial_for("tb.x", ToolbarVisibilityMode.COLLAPSIBLE)
    assert state.visible is True


def test_initial_for_auto_hide_is_hidden() -> None:
    state = ToolbarState.initial_for("tb.x", ToolbarVisibilityMode.AUTO_HIDE)
    assert state.visible is False


def test_initial_for_hidden_is_hidden() -> None:
    state = ToolbarState.initial_for("tb.x", ToolbarVisibilityMode.HIDDEN)
    assert state.visible is False


def test_initial_for_unknown_mode_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ToolbarStateError, match="unknown visibility mode"):
        ToolbarState.initial_for("tb.x", "not-a-mode")  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def test_state_is_mutable() -> None:
    state = ToolbarState(toolbar_id="tb.x", visible=True)
    state.visible = False
    assert state.visible is False
