"""Tests for AreaSpec."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.area_spec import AreaSpec, AreaSpecError
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.editor_layout_spec import EditorLayoutSpec


def _leaf(editor_id: str) -> EditorLayoutSpec:
    return EditorLayoutSpec.leaf(editor_id)


def test_area_spec_holds_fields() -> None:
    spec = AreaSpec(
        area_id="area.central",
        title="Central",
        placement=Placement.CENTRAL,
        editor_layout=_leaf("editor.a"),
    )
    assert spec.area_id == "area.central"
    assert spec.title == "Central"
    assert spec.placement is Placement.CENTRAL
    assert spec.editor_layout.editor_id == "editor.a"


def test_empty_area_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(AreaSpecError, match="area_id"):
        AreaSpec(
            area_id="",
            title="X",
            placement=Placement.CENTRAL,
            editor_layout=_leaf("editor.a"),
        )


def test_empty_title_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(AreaSpecError, match="title"):
        AreaSpec(
            area_id="area.x",
            title="",
            placement=Placement.CENTRAL,
            editor_layout=_leaf("editor.a"),
        )



======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/ui/workspace/specs/test_editor_layout_spec.py
======================================================================

"""Tests for EditorLayoutSpec."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.editor_layout_spec import (
    EditorLayoutSpec,
    EditorLayoutSpecError,
    LayoutType,
    SplitDirection,
)

# ----- LEAF ---------------------------------------------------------


def test_leaf_factory_creates_leaf() -> None:
    spec = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    assert spec.type is LayoutType.LEAF
    assert spec.editor_id == "editor.a"
    assert spec.direction is None
    assert spec.children == ()


def test_leaf_without_editor_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="editor_id"):
        EditorLayoutSpec(type=LayoutType.LEAF)


def test_leaf_with_children_raises() -> None:
    child = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="must not have children"):
        EditorLayoutSpec(
            type=LayoutType.LEAF,
            editor_id="editor.b",
            children=(child,),
        )


def test_leaf_with_direction_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="must not declare direction"):
        EditorLayoutSpec(
            type=LayoutType.LEAF,
            editor_id="editor.a",
            direction=SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
        )


# ----- SPLIT --------------------------------------------------------


def test_split_factory_creates_split() -> None:
    a = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    b = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.b")
    spec = EditorLayoutSpec.split(SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL, (a, b))
    assert spec.type is LayoutType.SPLIT
    assert spec.direction is SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL
    assert spec.children == (a, b)
    assert spec.editor_id is None


def test_split_with_editor_id_raises() -> None:
    a = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    b = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.b")
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="must not declare editor_id"):
        EditorLayoutSpec(
            type=LayoutType.SPLIT,
            direction=SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL,
            children=(a, b),
            editor_id="editor.c",
        )


def test_split_without_direction_raises() -> None:
    a = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    b = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.b")
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="must declare direction"):
        EditorLayoutSpec(
            type=LayoutType.SPLIT,
            children=(a, b),
        )


def test_split_with_one_child_raises() -> None:
    a = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="at least two children"):
        EditorLayoutSpec.split(SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL, (a,))


def test_split_with_zero_children_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="at least two children"):
        EditorLayoutSpec.split(SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL, ())


def test_nested_split_is_valid() -> None:
    """Replicates the prototype's 2x2 central area."""
    a = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.a")
    b = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.b")
    c = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.c")
    d = EditorLayoutSpec.leaf("editor.d")

    inner_left = EditorLayoutSpec.split(SplitDirection.VERTICAL, (a, b))
    inner_right = EditorLayoutSpec.split(SplitDirection.VERTICAL, (c, d))
    outer = EditorLayoutSpec.split(SplitDirection.HORIZONTAL, (inner_left, inner_right))

    assert outer.type is LayoutType.SPLIT
    assert len(outer.children) == 2
    assert outer.children[0].type is LayoutType.SPLIT
    assert outer.children[1].type is LayoutType.SPLIT


def test_unknown_layout_type_raises() -> None:
    class Fake:
        pass

    with pytest.raises(EditorLayoutSpecError, match="unknown layout type"):
        EditorLayoutSpec(type=Fake)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

FILE: tests/unit/ui/workspace/specs/test_module_ui_spec.py

"""Tests for ModuleUISpec."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.workspace.areas.placement import Placement
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.area_spec import AreaSpec
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.editor_layout_spec import EditorLayoutSpec
from cranioz.ui.workspace.specs.module_ui_spec import (
    ModuleUISpec,
    ModuleUISpecError,
)


def _area(area_id: str, placement: Placement, editor_id: str) -> AreaSpec:
    return AreaSpec(
        area_id=area_id,
        title=area_id,
        placement=placement,
        editor_layout=EditorLayoutSpec.leaf(editor_id),
    )


def test_single_central_is_valid() -> None:
    spec = ModuleUISpec(areas=(_area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.a"),))
    assert len(spec.areas) == 1
    assert spec.overlays == ()


def test_left_central_right_is_valid() -> None:
    spec = ModuleUISpec(
        areas=(
            _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
            _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
            _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
        )
    )
    assert len(spec.areas) == 3


def test_four_areas_is_valid() -> None:
    spec = ModuleUISpec(
        areas=(
            _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
            _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.central"),
            _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
            _area("area.bottom", Placement.BOTTOM, "editor.bottom"),
        )
    )
    assert len(spec.areas) == 4


def test_without_central_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ModuleUISpecError, match="CENTRAL"):
        ModuleUISpec(
            areas=(
                _area("area.left", Placement.LEFT, "editor.left"),
                _area("area.right", Placement.RIGHT, "editor.right"),
            )
        )


def test_duplicate_area_id_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ModuleUISpecError, match="unique"):
        ModuleUISpec(
            areas=(
                _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.a"),
                _area("area.central", Placement.CENTRAL, "editor.b"),
            )
        )


def test_empty_areas_raises() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ModuleUISpecError, match="at least one Area"):
        ModuleUISpec(areas=())

"""Tests for HomeSegment (Phase 2, §10.10)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.workspace.structure.home_segment import HomeSegment


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_toggle_area_left_button_emits_signal(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = HomeSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(segment.toggle_area_left_requested, timeout=1000):
        segment.findChild(
            type(segment._toggle_left_button),  # noqa: SLF001
            "workspace.top_bar.home_segment.toggle_left",
        ).click()


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_close_workspace_button_emits_signal(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = HomeSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(segment.close_workspace_requested, timeout=1000):
        segment.findChild(
            type(segment._close_workspace_button),  # noqa: SLF001
            "workspace.top_bar.home_segment.close_workspace",
        ).click()


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_menu_new_project_emits_signal(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = HomeSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(segment.new_project_requested, timeout=1000):
        segment._action_new_project.trigger()  # noqa: SLF001

def test_menu_open_project_emits_signal(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = HomeSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(segment.open_project_requested, timeout=1000):
        segment._action_open_project.trigger()  # noqa: SLF001

    segment = HomeSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)
    segment.set_project_open(True)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(segment.close_project_requested, timeout=1000):
        segment._action_close_project.trigger()  # noqa: SLF001


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_menu_import_dicom_emits_signal(qtbot) -> None:

    qtbot.addWidget(segment)
    segment.set_project_open(True)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(segment.import_files_requested, timeout=1000):
        segment._action_import_dicom.trigger()  # noqa: SLF001


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_menu_import_stl_debug_emits_signal(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = HomeSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)
    segment.set_project_open(True)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(segment.import_stl_debug_requested, timeout=1000):
        segment._action_import_stl.trigger()  # noqa: SLF001


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_context_actions_disabled_when_no_project(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = HomeSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)
    segment.set_project_open(False)

    assert not segment._action_close_project.isEnabled()  # noqa: SLF001
    assert not segment._import_menu.isEnabled()  # noqa: SLF001
    assert segment._action_new_project.isEnabled()  # noqa: SLF001
    assert segment._action_open_project.isEnabled()  # noqa: SLF001


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_context_actions_enabled_when_project_open(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = HomeSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)
    segment.set_project_open(True)

    assert segment._action_close_project.isEnabled()  # noqa: SLF001
    assert segment._import_menu.isEnabled()  # noqa: SLF001


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_project_open_state_round_trips(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = HomeSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)

    assert segment.is_project_open() is False
    segment.set_project_open(True)
    assert segment.is_project_open() is True
    segment.set_project_open(False)
    assert segment.is_project_open() is False

"""Tests for ModulesSegment (Phase 2, §10.10)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from cranioz.ui.workspace.structure.modules_segment import ModulesSegment


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_starts_empty(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = ModulesSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)

    assert segment.module_ids() == ()
    assert segment.selected_module_id() is None


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_add_button_emits_signal(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = ModulesSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)

    with qtbot.waitSignal(segment.add_module_requested, timeout=1000):
        segment._add_button.click()  # noqa: SLF001


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_set_modules_populates(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = ModulesSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)

    segment.set_modules((("tomography", "Tomography"), ("osteotomy", "Osteotomy")))

    assert segment.module_ids() == ("tomography", "osteotomy")


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_set_modules_replaces_existing(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = ModulesSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)

    segment.set_modules((("a", "A"), ("b", "B")))
    segment.set_modules((("c", "C"),))

    assert segment.module_ids() == ("c",)


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_clicking_module_emits_signal(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = ModulesSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)
    segment.set_modules((("tomography", "Tomography"),))

    with qtbot.waitSignal(segment.module_selected, timeout=1000) as blocker:
        segment._module_buttons["tomography"].click()  # noqa: SLF001

    assert blocker.args[0] == "tomography"
    assert segment.selected_module_id() == "tomography"


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_clicking_selected_module_deselects(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = ModulesSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)
    segment.set_modules((("tomography", "Tomography"),))

    # First click: select.
    segment._module_buttons["tomography"].click()  # noqa: SLF001
    assert segment.selected_module_id() == "tomography"

    # Second click: deselect.
    segment._module_buttons["tomography"].click()  # noqa: SLF001
    assert segment.selected_module_id() is None


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_set_selected_marks_button(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = ModulesSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)
    segment.set_modules((("a", "A"), ("b", "B")))

    segment.set_selected("b")

    assert segment.selected_module_id() == "b"
    assert segment._module_buttons["b"].isChecked() is True  # noqa: SLF001
    assert segment._module_buttons["a"].isChecked() is False  # noqa: SLF001


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_set_selected_none_clears(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = ModulesSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)
    segment.set_modules((("a", "A"),))
    segment.set_selected("a")

    segment.set_selected(None)

    assert segment.selected_module_id() is None
    assert segment._module_buttons["a"].isChecked() is False  # noqa: SLF001


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_set_selected_unknown_raises(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = ModulesSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)
    segment.set_modules((("a", "A"),))

    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="unknown module_id"):
        segment.set_selected("unknown")


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_set_modules_preserves_selection_when_present(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = ModulesSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)
    segment.set_modules((("a", "A"), ("b", "B")))
    segment.set_selected("b")

    segment.set_modules((("a", "A"), ("b", "B"), ("c", "C")))

    assert segment.selected_module_id() == "b"


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_set_modules_clears_selection_when_absent(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = ModulesSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)
    segment.set_modules((("a", "A"), ("b", "B")))
    segment.set_selected("b")

    segment.set_modules((("a", "A"),))

    assert segment.selected_module_id() is None


@pytest.mark.qt
def test_only_one_module_selected_at_a_time(qtbot) -> None:
    segment = ModulesSegment()
    qtbot.addWidget(segment)
    segment.set_modules((("a", "A"), ("b", "B")))

    segment.set_selected("a")
    segment.set_selected("b")

    assert segment.selected_module_id() == "b"
    assert segment._module_buttons["a"].isChecked() is False  # noqa: SLF001
    assert segment._module_buttons["b"].isChecked() is True  # noqa: SLF001
